{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"17c7aaae-2575-46c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"这是一个**只读研究任务**，不要修改任何文件。\n\n## 背景\n我在重写 LetShare（一个 WebRTC/WebSocket 实时协作应用）的核心层。这个仓库有一个关键特征：**只有 1 个 TODO/FIXME，但有 89 行\"踩坑教训\"注释**（关键词如 `根因`/`否则`/`必须`/`兜底`/`幂等`/`静音`/`单通`/`漏`/`竞态`）。这说明所有来之不易的知识都沉淀在注释里，而不是在文档或测试里。\n\n我已经把**通话/会议媒体链路**部分的坑整理成了 `rewrite/QUIRKS.md`（Q01–Q22）。现在需要你把**其余区域**的坑系统性地挖出来，因为重写时如果不知道这些坑，就会在原样重新踩一遍（症状通常是\"单通/双声/静音/黑屏/连不上/文件传不完\"）。\n\n## 要挖掘的区域（按优先级）\n1. `src/app/libs/connection/ServerFileTransfer.ts`（2218 行）\n2. `src/app/libs/connection/transferReliability.ts`（1982 行）\n3. `src/app/libs/connection/peerManager.ts`、`p2pHandshake.ts`\n4. `server/internal/service/file_transfer.go`（968 行）、`server/test/websocket_file_transfer.go`\n5. `src/components/meeting/MeetingRoom.tsx`（1930 行）\n6. `src/pages/share.tsx`（2827 行）—— 只看与连接/媒体/生命周期相关的部分\n7. `memory/*.md`（5 篇记忆文件）—— 逐条转成条目\n8. `server/internal/handler/websocket.go`（4832 行）—— 只看 meeting/file-transfer/连接生命周期相关的分支与注释\n\n## 怎么判断\"这是一个坑\"\n找这些信号：\n- 注释里出现解释性因果：「否则…会…」「…会导致…」「…的根因」「不能只…」「必须…才…」「曾…」「兜底」「防…」\n- 代码里有看起来\"多余\"但被注释保护的判断（例如浏览器兼容、时序竞态、重试、幂等、去重、上限）\n- 常量里出现\"魔法数字\"且注释解释了它的来源（超时、上限、延迟）\n- 同一逻辑在两处出现但实现**不一致**（这类是高价值发现，请特别标注）\n\n## 输出格式（严格遵守）\n按下面的表格输出，**每条必须带 file:line**，否则不算发现：\n\n| 坑（一句话，可断言的行为）| 出处 file:line | 为什么（会导致什么症状）| 严重度 高/中/低 |\n|---|---|---|---|\n\n额外再输出两个小节：\n- **「拷贝分叉」清单**：同一逻辑在多处实现且实现不一致的地方（这是重写最危险的来源）\n- **「可疑魔法数字」清单**：超时/上限/延迟常量，标注是否有注释解释来源\n\n## 约束\n- 只读：不要修改任何文件\n- 不要泛泛而谈\"代码质量差\"，每条都要是可验证的具体行为\n- 宁少勿滥：给 25–40 条高质量的，不要凑数\n- 如果某个区域的坑与媒体/连接/生命周期无关（纯 UI 样式、i18n 文案），直接跳过"},"timestamp":"2026-10-06T19:44:50.765Z","cwd":"D:\\MyFile\\sample\\synchronous-github\\LetShare"}

{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"17c7aaae-2575-46c","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_00_MbO9P5vxjYsk6cQydMlX5597","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"=== memory/file-message-support.md ===\n     1\t---\n     2\tname: file-message-support\n     3\tdescription: Chat panel now supports file messages - send, receive, persist, download, and batch delete\n     4\tmetadata:\n     5\t  type: project\n     6\t---\n     7\t\n     8\t# File Message Support in Chat Panel\n     9\t\n    10\t## What was built\n    11\tAdded complete file message support to the chat panel, unifying the previously separate file transfer system with chat messaging. Users can now send files directly from the chat panel, see file bubbles with progress bars, download received files, and manage files in the Download panel.\n    12\t\n    13\t## Key files changed/created\n    14\t\n    15\t### New files:\n    16\t- `src/app/libs/chat/FileBlobStore.ts` — IndexedDB blob storage, uses ChatHistoryManager's shared DB\n    17\t- `src/components/Chat/FileBubble.tsx` — File message bubble (icon + name + size + progress + download)\n    18\t- `src/components/Chat/ImageBubble.tsx` — Image message bubble (thumbnail preview + fullscreen viewer)\n    19\t\n    20\t### Modified files:\n    21\t- `src/app/libs/chat/ChatHistoryManager.ts` — Extended ChatMessage to discriminated union (TextChatMessage | FileChatMessage), added addFileMessage(), updateFileMessageProgress(), deleteMessage(), getAllFileMessages(). DB version bumped to 2.\n    22\t- `src/app/libs/chat/ChatIntegration.ts` — Added file message bridge: sendFileMessage(), handleFileSent(), handleFileReceived(). Listens to file-sent/file-received events from colabLib.\n    23\t- `src/components/Chat/ChatPanel.tsx` — Completed handleFileSelect (was TODO stub), added type-based message rendering (FileBubble/ImageBubble for file messages), added paste-to-send-file support.\n    24\t- `src/app/libs/connection/colabLib.ts` — Extended ColabEvents type with file-sent and file-received events. Emits them on P2P and server transfer completion.\n    25\t- `src/components/Download.tsx` — Added per-user file grouping, collapsible sections, batch select/delete, select-all, clear-all.\n    26\t- `src/app/libs/i18n/translation.ts` — Added 14 new i18n keys across en/zh/ms/id.\n    27\t- `src/types/index.d.ts` — Added FileCategory and FileTransferStatus types.\n    28\t- `vite.config.ts` — SW cache bumped to v9 (bumped to v10 in v3.4.0).\n    29\t\n    30\t## Architecture decisions\n    31\t- File blobs stored in IndexedDB (same DB as chat history, shared connection via ChatHistoryManager.getDB())\n    32\t- ChatMessage is now a discriminated union: TextChatMessage | FileChatMessage\n    33\t- File messages have a FileMetadata sub-object with transferStatus tracking\n    34\t- DB version centralized in ChatHistoryManager (single onupgradeneeded creates both stores)\n    35\t- File transfer events (file-sent/file-received) bridge the gap between transfer system and chat system\n    36\t\n    37\t## Known limitations\n    38\t- Sent files not re-downloadable by sender (no blob storage for outgoing files)\n    39\t- No retry mechanism for failed uploads (original File object not preserved)\n    40\t- Progress updates not yet hooked from transfer system to chat bubbles (placeholder created, completed event updates)\n    41\t\n    42\t## Related\n    43\tSee documents/PRD-file-message-support.md and documents/IMPLEMENTATION-PLAN-file-messages.md\n=== memory/push-checklist.md ===\n     1\t---\n     2\tname: push-checklist\n     3\tdescription: 发版标准流程：deploy.cjs 一条命令、docs/ 产物同步、版本号 bump、--no-verify 陷阱、双端同步时序\n     4\tmetadata:\n     5\t  type: feedback\n     6\t---\n     7\t\n     8\t# 发版/部署标准清单（2026-09-01 用户要求；2026-09-02 随 ECS 源站架构更新）\n     9\t\n    10\t**Why:** 曾发生三种事故：只改源码不重建 docs/ 就 push（Pages 与源码不一致）；版本号（package.json + mobx.ts）漏 bump；`--no-verify` 跳过 pre-push 钩子后忘记补 docs/ 产物同步（海外滞后一版）。\n    11\t\n    12\t**How to apply:**\n    13\t1. **发版一条命令（已标准化）**：bump 版本（`package.json` + `src/app/libs/mobx/mobx.ts`）→ `node scripts/deploy.cjs --frontend`。脚本自动完成：Vite 构建 → 部署 ECS nginx 回源（大陆 ~1min 生效）→ CDN 刷新（需 `ALIYUN_ACCESS_KEY_ID/SECRET` env，缺省跳过并提示）→ docs/ 产物 commit+push（海外 Pages ~2-3min 生效）→ 健康检查。后端单独：`node scripts/deploy.cjs --backend`。\n    14\t2. **手工 `git push --no-verify` 后必须补产物同步**：`git add docs && git commit && git push --no-verify`（pre-push 钩子被跳过时构建/同步都不发生，Pages 部署的是旧产物）。deploy.cjs 的 `syncDocs()` 已兜底此步；pre-push 钩子现已内置 **docs/ 新鲜度闸门**（build 后 docs 有未提交变更即阻断 push 并打印补救命令），正常走钩子不可能漏同步。E2E 已移出 pre-push（仍不在 CI，需本地手动 `pnpm test:e2e:call`）。\n    15\t3. SW 缓存名只在缓存策略变化时改（见 [[sw-cache-version-bump]]）。\n    16\t4. CI 对齐：`.github/workflows/ci.yml` pnpm=11 要求 Node >=22.13（依赖 node:sqlite）。\n    17\t5. push 后 `gh run list` 验 CI（需 `HTTPS_PROXY=http://127.0.0.1:7897`）；外网直测用 `curl.exe --noproxy \"*\"`（本机 Clash 代理 env 会干扰直连）。\n    18\t6. **本机直连 GitHub 已不通（连接被重置）**：仓库已设 `http.proxy=http://127.0.0.1:7897`（`verge-mihomo` 实际监听 7897；**7898 未监听**，用户曾提 7898 但实测不通）。`gh` CLI 不吃 http.proxy，仍需显式 `HTTPS_PROXY=http://127.0.0.1:7897`。\n    19\t7. PowerShell 5.1 改文件用 `[System.IO.File]::WriteAllText` + `UTF8Encoding($false)`——`Set-Content -Encoding UTF8` 会写 BOM，打崩 vite-plugin-pwa 的 JSON.parse。\n    20\t8. 服务器端口归属（ECS）：nginx `:80`（letshare.fun 静态回源，**禁 301**，CDN 回源跟随会死循环）+ `:18081`（备用）；Go `:8080`（HTTP→HTTPS 跳转）+ `:443`（TLS）+ `:3478`（TURN）。nginx 模板：`scripts/letshare-static.nginx.conf`；全程记录：`Harness/project/2026-09-02-letshare-cdn-origin-ecs.md`。\n=== memory/server-relay-complete-before-end.md ===\n     1\t---\n     2\tname: server-relay-complete-before-end\n     3\tdescription: Server relay ACK flow control fix for COMPLETE arriving before sender END\n     4\tmetadata:\n     5\t  type: project\n     6\t---\n     7\t\n     8\t# Server Relay COMPLETE Before END\n     9\t\n    10\tOn 2026-07-01, production relay logs showed `传输会话状态错误，无法确认完成: transferring`.\n    11\t\n    12\tRoot cause: the ACK/backpressure server accepted `file:transfer:complete` only from `ending` or `resending`, but a receiver can finish assembly and send COMPLETE before the sender's END advances the server session out of `transferring`.\n    13\t\n    14\tFix: server commit `bb5911b` allows `transferring`, `ending`, and `resending` to transition to `completed` in `handleFileTransferComplete`.\n    15\t\n    16\tVerification used:\n    17\t- `go test ./cmd/... ./internal/... ./pkg/...`\n    18\t- `go test -tags letshare_live ./internal/handler -run TestLive_HappyPath -count=1 -v`\n    19\t- Direct WSS probe where receiver sent COMPLETE before sender END and sender received `file:transfer:complete`.\n    20\t\n    21\tDeployment: ECS `/root/cloud/letshare-server-9-linux` was rebuilt from `bb5911b` and restarted. The pre-fix rollback binary was backed up as `/root/cloud/letshare-server-9-linux.bak.before_bb5911b_20260701_170749`.\n=== memory/server-relay-stale-client-reset.md ===\n     1\t---\n     2\tname: server-relay-stale-client-reset\n     3\tdescription: Server relay stale websocket reset handling for deterministic transfer failures\n     4\tmetadata:\n     5\t  type: project\n     6\t---\n     7\t\n     8\t# Server Relay Stale Client Reset\n     9\t\n    10\tOn 2026-07-27, server relay transfers could fail repeatedly with `write: connection reset by peer` while forwarding chunks. The likely failure mode was a stale receiver websocket remaining selectable in the room after reconnect/reset, so retries kept writing relay chunks to a dead connection.\n    11\t\n    12\tFix pattern:\n    13\t- Treat reset/broken/timeout websocket write errors as closed or unusable.\n    14\t- Remove stale clients after direct relay control or binary writes fail with a closed/unusable connection.\n    15\t- Prefer the receiver `clientID` recorded during `file:transfer:accept` before falling back to user lookup.\n    16\t- Retry same-user control messages after removing a stale socket, and rebind relay chunks to a newer active receiver socket if the accepted socket is stale.\n    17\t- Send protocol-critical `file:transfer:error` messages outside the generic error limiter so the active peer is not left waiting.\n    18\t\n    19\tVerification used:\n    20\t- `go test ./internal/service -run \"TestWriteErrorClassification|TestSendMessageToUserRemovesClosedClient|TestForwardChunkToReceiverRemovesClosedReceiverClient|TestHandlerRefreshesReadDeadline\" -count=1 -v`\n    21\t- `go test ./internal/handler -run TestNotifyTransferErrorBypassesGenericRateLimiter -count=1 -v`\n    22\t- `go test ./cmd/... ./internal/... ./pkg/...`\n    23\t- `go test -race ./cmd/... ./internal/... ./pkg/...`\n    24\t- `go build ./cmd/server`\n    25\t\n    26\tReleased on 2026-07-27:\n    27\t- Backend commit `80f71c9` was pushed to `LiWeny16/letshare_server`.\n    28\t- `/root/cloud/letshare-server-linux` on `ecs.letshare.fun` was replaced with SHA256 `2baf2cffc7bcb1ad7987b6e6af304b67eda578feadac60da6e5d600971d0c40b`.\n    29\t- `letshare.service` restarted successfully and local ECS `https://127.0.0.1/health` returned healthy.\n    30\t- Frontend deployment bump: version `3.5.3`, `external-cache-v18`.\n=== memory/sw-cache-version-bump.md ===\n     1\t---\r\n     2\tname: sw-cache-version-bump\r\n     3\tdescription: Any JS/chunk/PWA change MUST bump SW cache name in vite.config.ts; version.json sentinel auto-detects new deploys\r\n     4\tmetadata:\r\n     5\t  type: project\r\n     6\t---\r\n     7\t\r\n     8\t# SW Cache + Version Sentinel Strategy\r\n     9\t\r\n    10\t## Version Sentinel (version.json)\r\n    11\t\r\n    12\tThe build generates `docs/version.json` with a unique build ID (`scripts/generate-version-json.cjs`).  \r\n    13\tClients check this file on every page load and tab-visibility change.  \r\n    14\tIf the version changed → SW is unregistered, caches cleared, page reloaded → instant update.  \r\n    15\tIf the version matches → all caches valid, app loads instantly.\r\n    16\t\r\n    17\t**No manual cache version bump needed** for runtime caches — the sentinel handles detection.\r\n    18\t\r\n    19\t## Cache Strategy (vite.config.ts)\r\n    20\t\r\n    21\t| Cache | Strategy | TTL | Rationale |\r\n    22\t|-------|----------|-----|-----------|\r\n    23\t| `version-sentinel` | NetworkFirst | 0 (no cache) | Must always hit server |\r\n    24\t| `html-cache-v3` | StaleWhileRevalidate | 365 days | Sentinel triggers refresh on version change |\r\n    25\t| `external-cache-v15` | StaleWhileRevalidate | 365 days | Same — sentinel invalidates when needed |\r\n    26\t\r\n    27\t## Version String Sync Checklist\r\n    28\t\r\n    29\tWhen bumping the LetShare version (e.g. 3.5.7 → 3.5.8), update ALL of these:\r\n    30\t\r\n    31\t1. **`package.json`** → `\"version\"` field\r\n    32\t2. **`src/app/libs/mobx/mobx.ts`** → `DEFAULT_SETTINGS.version` (hardcoded string, shown in Footer)\r\n    33\t3. **Commit, push, tag** → `git tag v{version}` + `gh release create`\r\n    34\t\r\n    35\tDo NOT skip #2 — it is not derived from package.json at runtime. The Footer reads `settingsStore.get(\"version\")` which defaults to this hardcoded value.\r\n    36\t\r\n    37\t## When to Bump Cache Names\r\n    38\t\r\n    39\tOnly bump `html-cache-v{N}` or `external-cache-v{N}` when the **caching strategy** changes (not the content).\r\n    40\tThe version.json sentinel handles content-change detection automatically.\r\n    41\t\r\n    42\t## Automatic Cleanup\r\n    43\t\r\n    44\t`scripts/cleanup-old-chunks.cjs` runs as `prebuild`. Removes old hashed chunks not referenced in current HTML.\r\n    45\tDynamic-import chunks (AblyConnectionProvider) are preserved.\r\n    46\t\r\n    47\tRun `npm run clean` to manually clean old chunks without a full build.\r\n"}],"isError":false,"timestamp":1791315895660},"timestamp":"2026-10-06T19:44:55.664Z","cwd":"D:\\MyFile\\sample\\synchronous-github\\LetShare"}

/**\n  * 设置消息处理器\n  */\n private setupMessageHandlers() {\n  // 这些处理器需要在ConnectionManager中注册\n  // 我们暴露一个方法供外部调用\n }\n\n /**\n  * 处理收到的文件传输消息\n  */\n public handleFileTransferMessage(type: string, data: any) {\n  console.debug(`[ServerFileTransfer] Received message type: ${type}`, data);\n\n  // 处理旧版 \"error\" 类型消息(服务器 sendError 使用的旧格式, 不含 transfer_id 顶层字段)\n  if (type === \"error\") {\n   const errorPayload = data?.error || data;\n   const errorMsg = errorPayload?.message || errorPayload?.error || t('alert.unknownError');\n   console.error(`[ServerFileTransfer] Server error:`, errorMsg);\n   const errTransferId = errorPayload?.transfer_id || (data as any)?.transfer_id;\n   if (errTransferId) {\n    const sendingSession = this.sendingSessions.get(errTransferId);\n    if (sendingSession) {\n     const wasPending = sendingSession.status === \"pending\";\n     sendingSession.status = \"error\";\n     this.sendingSessions.delete(errTransferId);\n     this.clearTransferTimeout(errTransferId);\n     if (this.currentSendingTransferId === errTransferId) {\n      this.currentSendingTransferId = null;\n     }\n     if (wasPending) {\n      this.completionAcks.cancel(errTransferId);\n     } else {\n      this.completionAcks.reject(errTransferId, new Error(errorMsg));\n     }\n     this.onProgressCallback?.(null);\n     this.setTransferStatus(errorMsg, \"error\");\n     this.rejectSendCompletion(errTransferId, new Error(errorMsg));\n    }\n    const receivingSession = this.receivingSessions.get(errTransferId);\n    if (receivingSession) {\n     this.failReceiveSession(receivingSession, errorMsg, FILE_TRANSFER_MESSAGE_TYPES.ERROR);\n    }\n   } else {\n   // 无 transfer_id — 通用错误, 展示给用户\n   this.setTransferStatus(errorMsg, \"error\");\n   alertUseMUI(errorMsg, 4000, { kind: \"error\", category: \"transfer-status\" });\n   }\n   return;\n  }\n\n  const normalizedPayload = normalizeTransferControlPayload(data);\n  if (!normalizedPayload.valid) {\n   this.handleMalformedTransferMessage(type, data, normalizedPayload.reason);\n   return;\n  }\n\n  const payload = normalizedPayload.payload as any;\n\n  void runTransferHandlerSafely(async () => {\n   switch (type) {\n    case FILE_TRANSFER_MESSAGE_TYPES.REQUEST:\n     await this.handleTransferRequest(payload);\n     break;\n    case FILE_TRANSFER_MESSAGE_TYPES.ACCEPT:\n     await this.handleTransferAccept(payload);\n     break;\n    case FILE_TRANSFER_MESSAGE_TYPES.REJECT:\n     this.handleTransferReject(payload);\n     break;\n    case FILE_TRANSFER_MESSAGE_TYPES.START:\n     this.handleTransferStart(payload);\n     break;\n    case FILE_TRANSFER_MESSAGE_TYPES.CHUNK:\n     this.handleTransferChunk(payload);\n     break;\n    case FILE_TRANSFER_MESSAGE_TYPES.END:\n     this.handleTransferEnd(payload);\n     break;\n    case FILE_TRANSFER_MESSAGE_TYPES.COMPLETE:\n     this.handleTransferComplete(payload);\n     break;\n    case FILE_TRANSFER_MESSAGE_TYPES.ACK:\n     this.handleTransferAck(payload);\n     break;\n    case FILE_TRANSFER_MESSAGE_TYPES.RESEND:\n     await this.handleTransferResend(payload);\n     break;\n    case FILE_TRANSFER_MESSAGE_TYPES.RESUME_STATE:\n     this.handleTransferResumeState(payload);\n     break;\n    case FILE_TRANSFER_MESSAGE_TYPES.CANCEL:\n     this.handleTransferCancel(payload);\n     break;\n    case FILE_TRANSFER_MESSAGE_TYPES.ERROR:\n     this.handleTransferError(payload);\n     break;\n    case FILE_TRANSFER_MESSAGE_TYPES.PROGRESS:\n     this.handleTransferProgress(payload);\n     break;\n   }\n  }, (error) => {\n   this.handleMalformedTransferMessage(type, payload, error);\n  });\n }\n\n /**\n  * 处理二进制文件块数据 — 直接写入预分配缓冲区, O(1) 无额外拷贝\n  */\n public handleBinaryData(data: ArrayBuffer) {\n  console.debug(`[ServerFileTransfer] Received binary data: ${data.byteLength} bytes`);\n\n  try {\n   const { meta, payload } = decodeTransferFrame(data);\n   if (this.completedTransferIds.has(meta.transfer_id)) {\n    console.debug(`[ServerFileTransfer] Ignoring late chunk for completed transfer=${meta.transfer_id}`);\n    return;\n   }\n   const session = this.receivingSessions.get(meta.transfer_id);\n   if (!session || session.status !== \"receiving\") {\n    const reason = t('alert.chunkWithoutTransfer');\n    console.warn(`[ServerFileTransfer] ${reason} transfer=${meta.transfer_id}`);\n    if (shouldReportTransferIssueOnce(this.unknownBinaryTransferIssueKeys, meta.transfer_id)) {\n     this.sendTransferControlMessage(\n      FILE_TRANSFER_MESSAGE_TYPES.CANCEL,\n      meta.transfer_id,\n      reason\n     );\n     this.onProgressCallback?.(null);\n     this.setTransferStatus(reason, \"error\");\n     alertUseMUI(reason, 4000, { kind: \"error\", category: \"transfer-status\" });\n    }\n    return;\n   }\n ignored: ${chunkIndex} transfer=${session.transferId}`);\n   return;\n  }\n\n  const bytes = data instanceof ArrayBuffer\n   ? new Uint8Array(data)\n   : new Uint8Array(data.buffer, data.byteOffset, t('alert.chunkCorrupted'));\n   return;\n  }\n\n  // 双模式写入：浏览器缓存优先 buffer（快），超限文件直接写入用户选择的磁盘文件。\n  if (session.storageMode === \"direct-to-disk\") {\n   if (!session.directSink) {\n    this.failReceiveSession(session, t('alert.bufferNotAvailable'));\n    return;\n   }\n   const writeResult = await session.directSink.writeChunk(chunkIndex, bytes);\n   if (!writeResult.accepted) {\n session.resendAttempts = 0;\n  this.maybeSendReceiverAck(session, chunkIndex);\n\n   const progress = (session.receivedCount / session.totalChunks) * 100;\n   this.onProgressCallback?.(progress);\n   this.emitReceivingUpdate(session, \"transferring\", progress);\n\n  if (session.receivedCount % 50 === 0 || session.receivedCount === session.totalChunks) {\n   console.debug(`[ServerFileTransfer] * p2pHandshake — P2P 通道验证（probe）与传输 ACK 节拍的纯逻辑。\n *\n * 3.8.2 状态机（见 task-meeting-3-8-2-collab-hardening WF-006）：\n *   ICE connected → DataChannel open → probe → probe ACK → 测试数据 ACK\n *   → 正式传输 → 分块 ACK → completion ACK → 接收端 hash 校验 → 才显示 P2P 成功\n *\n * 本文件只做无副作用决策，colabLib 负责实际收发 —— 便于单元测试。\n */\n\nimport { IncrementalSha256 } from \"./sha256\";\n\n/** probe 帧头长度与 transfer frame 一致（256B 头 + 载荷），保持二进制路径单一。 */\nconst PROBE_FRAME_HEADER_SIZE = 256;\n\n// ── DataChannel 控制消息（字符串帧）──────────────────────────\n\nexport type P2PControlMessage =\n  | { type: \"file-probe\"; probeId: string }\n  | { type: \"file-probe-ack\"; probeId: string }\n  | { type: \"file-test-ack\"; probeId: string }\n  | { type: \"file-chunk-ack\"; transferId: string; receivedChunks: number; totalChunks: number; bytesReceived: number }\n  | { type: \"file-hash\"; transferId: string; hash: string }\n  | { type: \"file-hash-failed\"; transferId: string; expected: string; actual: string };\n\nexport function encodeP2PControl(message: P2PControlMessage): string {\n  return JSON.stringify(message);\n}\n\n// ── 通道验证（channel probe）────────────────────────────────\n\nexport interface ChannelVerificationState {\n  /** 控制（文本）帧回环是否已确认。 */\n  controlAcked: boolean;\n  /** 二进制帧回环是否已确认。 */\n  binaryAcked: boolean;\n  startedAt: number;\n}\n\nexport interface ChannelVerificationDecision {\n  verified: boolean;\n  timedOut: boolean;\n}\n\n/**\n * 判定通道验证结果：probe ACK 与 测试数据 ACK 都到达才算 verified；\n * 超时（PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS）内未双确认 → timedOut（P2P 不可用，UI 不得显示直连）。\n */\nexport function decideChannelVerification(\n  state: ChannelVerificationState,\n  now: number,\n  timeoutMs: number\n): ChannelVerificationDecision {\n  if (state.controlAcked && state.binaryAcked) {\n    return { verified: true, timedOut: false };\n  }\n  if (now - state.startedAt >= timeoutMs) {\n    return { verified: false, timedOut: true };\n  }\n  return { verified: false, timedOut: false };\n}\n\n/** 生成 probe 探测帧（二进制）：256B 头 JSON + probeId 载荷，probe 标记使其不进入分块流水线。 */\nexport function encodeProbeFrame(probeId: string): ArrayBuffer {\n  const payload = new TextEncoder().encode(probeId);\n  const header = JSON.stringify({ probe: true, probeId });\n  if (new TextEncoder().encode(header).byteLength > PROBE_FRAME_HEADER_SIZE) {\n    throw new Error(\"probe header too large\");\n  }\n  const frame = new Uint8Array(PROBE_FRAME_HEADER_SIZE + payload.byteLength);\n  frame.set(new TextEncoder().encode(header), 0);\n  frame.set(payload, PROBE_FRAME_HEADER_SIZE);\n  return frame.buffer;\n}\n\n/** 探测帧识别：header 里有 probe 标记且非 transfer 分块。 */\nexport function decodeProbeFrame(frame: ArrayBuffer): { probeId: string } | null {\n  try {\n    const bytes = new Uint8Array(frame);\n    if (bytes.byteLength < PROBE_FRAME_HEADER_SIZE) return null;\n    const headerBytes = bytes.subarray(0, PROBE_FRAME_HEADER_SIZE);\n    const headerEnd = headerBytes.indexOf(0);\n    const rawHeader = new TextDecoder().decode(\n      headerEnd === -1 ? headerBytes : headerBytes.subarray(0, headerEnd)\n    );\n    const meta = JSON.parse(rawHeader) as Record<string, unknown>;\n    if (meta.probe !== true || typeof meta.probeId !== \"string\" || !meta.probeId) return null;\n    return { probeId: meta.probeId };\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\n// ── 分块 ACK 节拍（接收端 → 发送端）────────────────────────\n\nexport interface ChunkAckPacingState {\n  receivedCount: number;\n  lastAckedCount: number;\n  lastAckAt: number;\n}\n\nexport interface ChunkAckDecision {\n  shouldAck: boolean;\n}\n\n/**\n * 接收端分块 ACK 节拍：每 ackEveryChunks 块，或距上次 ACK 超过 ackIntervalMs\n * 且尚有未确认进度时发送 ACK。保证发送端在 ACK_STALL_TIMEOUT 内必有进度信号。\n */\nexport function decideChunkAck(\n  state: ChunkAckPacingState,\n  now: number,\n  ackEveryChunks: number,\n  ackIntervalMs: number\n): ChunkAckDecision {\n  if (state.receivedCount === state.lastAckedCount) {\n    return { shouldAck: false };\n  }\n  if (state.receivedCount - state.lastAckedCount >= ackEveryChunks) {\n    return { shouldAck: true };\n  }\n  if (now - state.lastAckAt >= ackIntervalMs && state.receivedCount > state.lastAckedCount) {\n    return { shouldAck: true };\n  }\n  return { shouldAck: false };\n}\n\n// ── 发送端 ACK 看门狗（stall 检测）──────────────────────────\n\nexport interface SenderAckWatchdogState {\n  lastAckedBytes: number;\n  lastAckAdvanceAt: number;\n  lastLocalSendAt: number;\n}\n\nexport type SenderAckWatchdogDecision =\n  | { stalled: false }\n  | { stalled: true; reason: \"no-ack-progress\" | \"no-ack-at-all\" };\n\n/**\n * 发送端 stall 检测：传输已开始（有本地发送活动）但 ackedBytes 在\n * stallTimeoutMs 内没有任何增长 → 判定 P2P 卡死（本地 send() 成功不代表对端收到）。\n * 覆盖「文件卡在 21%、58.6%」的静默丢包场景。\n */\nexport function decideSenderAckWatchdog(\n  state: SenderAckWatchdogState,\n  now: number,\n  stallTimeoutMs: number,\n  transferStartedAt: number\n): SenderAckWatchdogDecision {\n  if (state.lastAckedBytes > 0) {\n    if (now - state.lastAckAdvanceAt >= stallTimeoutMs) {\n      return { stalled: true, reason: \"no-ack-progress\" };\n    }\n    return { stalled: false };\n  }\n  // 尚未收到任何 ACK：从首个本地发送起给一个完整的窗口\n  if (state.lastLocalSendAt > 0 && now - Math.max(transferStartedAt, state.lastLocalSendAt) >= stallTimeoutMs) {\n    return { stalled: true, reason: \"no-ack-at-all\" };\n  }\n  return { stalled: false };\n}\n\n// ── 接收端 hash 校验 ────────────────────────────────────────\n\nexport interface ReceiverHashCheck {\n  expected?: string;\n  hasher: IncrementalSha256;\n}\n\nexport function checkReceiverHash(check: ReceiverHashCheck): { ok: boolean; actual?: string } {\n  if (!check.expected) {\n    return { ok: true };\n  }\n  const actual = check.hasher.digestHex();\n  return { ok: actual === check.expected, actual };\n}\n\n// ── 自动 relay 切换决策 ─────────────────────────────────────\n\nexport interface P2PFallbackCandidate {\n  errorMessage: string;\n  userCancelled: boolean;\n  serverRelayAvailable: boolean;\n}\n\nexport interface P2PFallbackDecision {\n  fallbackToRelay: boolean;\n  reason?: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * P2P 失败后是否自动切换公网 relay：\n * - 用户主动取消 → 不切换（不重复发送）\n * - 服务器不可用（Ably 信令 / 无二进制通道）→ 无法切换\n * - 其余失败（probe 失败 / ACK 卡死 / hash 失败 / 通道断开）→ 自动 relay\n */\nexport function decideP2PRelayFallback(candidate: P2PFallbackCandidate): P2PFallbackDecision {\n  if (candidate.userCancelled) {\n    return { fallbackToRelay: false, reason: \"user-cancelled\" };\n  }\n  if (!candidate.serverRelayAvailable) {\n    return { fallbackToRelay: false, reason: \"relay-unavailable\" };\n  }\n  return { fallbackToRelay: true, reason: candidate.errorMessage };\n}\n\n[1 more lines in file. => {\n   /* 可扩展 */\n  };\n\n  let iceBuffer: RTCIceCandidate[] = [];\n  let isProcessing = false;\n\n  peer.onicecandidate = (event) => {\n   if (event.candidate) {\n    const staticIp = settingsStore.getUnrmb(\"staticIp\");\n    iceBuffer.push(event.candidate);\n    const cand = event.candidate;\n    const candidateStr = cand.candidate;\n    const parts = candidateStr.split(\" \");\n    const ip = parts[4];\n    const type = parts[7];\n    // 新增：比对和公网 IP\n    if (staticIp && ip !== staticIp && type === \"srflx\") {\n     alertUseMUI(t(\"alert.proxy\"));\n    }\n    if (!isProcessing) {\n     isProcessing = true;\n     setTimeout(() => {\n      this.rtc.broadcastSignal({\n       type: \"candidate\",\n       candidates: iceBuffer,\n       to: id,\n      });\n      iceBuffer = [];\n      isProcessing = false;\n     }, 400);\n    }\n   }\n  };\n\n  peer.ondatachannel = (event) => {\n   this.rtc.setupDataChannel(event.channel, id);\n  };\n   peer.onconnectionstatechange = () => {\n    console.debug(`[CONNECT] ${id} 状态:`, peer.connectionState);\n\n    if (peer.connectionState === \"connected\") {\n     console.debug(`[CONNECT] ${id} ICE connected，取消超时`);\n     // 3.8.2：ICE connected ≠ P2P 可用。用户状态保持 connecting，\n     // 等 DataChannel open + probe 双确认（colabLib.startChannelVerification）\n     // 才升级 connected —— 否则会把「ICE 通了但 SCTP 数据不通」显示成 P2P 成功。\n     const user = this.rtc.userList.get(id);\n     if (user && user.status !== \"connected\") {\n      user.status = \"connecting\";\n      this.rtc.userList.set(id, user);\n     }\n     clearTimeout(this.rtc.connectionTimeouts.get(id));\n    }\n\n   if ([\"failed\", \"disconnected\", \"closed\"].includes(peer.connectionState)) {\n    console.warn(`[CONNECT] ${id} 连接失败或断开`);\n    \n    const user = this.rtc.userList.get(id);\n    \n    // 如果用户曾经成功建立过P2P连接，断开时很可能是真的离线了\n    if (user?.hadP2PConnection && peer.connectionState === \"disconnected\") {\n     console.debug(`[CONNECT] ${id} had P2P connection before, likely offline, removing user`);\n     this.rtc.clearCache(id, { clearEncryption: true });\n     this.rtc.userList.delete(id);\n    } else {\n     // 如果从未建立过P2P连接，可能只是连接失败，降级到text-only\n     console.debug(`[CONNECT] ${id} P2P failed, switching to text-only mode`);\n     this.rtc.clearCache(id);\n     if (user) {\n      user.status = \"text-only\";\n      user.lastSeen = Date.now();\n      this.rtc.userList.set(id, user);\n     }\n    }\n    \n    peer.close();\n    RealTimeColab.peers.delete(id);\n    this.rtc.updateConnectedUsers(this.rtc.userList);\n   }\n  };\n \n  if (id) {\n   RealTimeColab.peers.set(id, peer);\n  }\n  return peer;\n }\n\n public removePeer(id: string): void {\n  this.rtc.clearCache(id, { clearEncryption: true });\n  this.rtc.negotiationMap.delete(id);\n  this.rtc.lastConnectAttempt.delete(id);\n  this.rtc.userList.delete(id);\n  console.debug(` 已清除与 ${id} 的连接资源`);\n }\n public isPrivateIP(ip: string) {\n  return (\n   ip.startsWith(\"10.\") ||\n   ip.startsWith(\"192.168.\") ||\n   ip.match(/^172\\.(1[6-9]|2\\d|3[0-1])\\./)\n  );\n }\n}\n\n[1 more {"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"17c7aaae-2575-46c","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_00_YgwgEWHGvuNBEicSNlh38222","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"\t// 写缓冲使用共享池，避免每连接固定分配 64KB\n\tWriteBufferPool: wsWriteBufferPool,\n}\n\nconst websocketReadTimeout = 120 * time.Second\n\nconst maxRelayResendChunkIndexes = 256\n\n// ErrorRateLimiter 限制向客户端发送错误消息的频率\n// 防止移动网络重连风暴时大量错误消息淹没客户端\ntype ErrorRateLimiter struct {\n\tmu      sync.Mutex\n\tclients map[string]*clientErrorRecord\n}\n\ntype clientErrorRecord struct {\n\ttimestamps   []time.Time\n\tblockedUntil time.Time\n}\n\n// NewErrorRateLimiter 创建错误频率限制器\nfunc NewErrorRateLimiter() *ErrorRateLimiter {\n\treturn &ErrorRateLimiter{\n\t\tclients: make(map[string]*clientErrorRecord),\n\t}\n}\n\n// Allow 检查是否允许向指定客户端发送错误消息\n// 5秒内超过3条错误则屏蔽10秒，避免重连风暴\nfunc (r *ErrorRateLimiter) Allow(clientID string) bool {\n\tr.mu.Lock()\n// client only includes file data when it changes; subsequent strokes stay\n// compact and do not repeatedly broadcast the image bytes.\nconst meetingExcalidrawMaxSceneBytes = 4 * 1024 * 1024\n\nconst meetingExcalidrawOperationCacheSize = 2048\n\nconst meetingChatHistoryMax = 200\n\n// hostLeaveGrace 房主意外断线（刷新/网络抖动）后等待其重入的宽限期。\n// 到点仍未回到房间则结束会议并释放资源，防止僵尸会议占用会议号与 SFU 内存。\n// hostLeaveGrace is deliberately long enough to cover a mobile Wi-Fi handoff,\n// browser background resume, or a short reconnect storm. An explicit leave is\n// still immediate; this grace only applies to an unexpected transport loss.\nvar hostLeaveGrace = 2 * time.Minute\n\n// meetingReservationTTL bounds a created-but-never-joined meeting. Once a\n// participant joins, the reservation timer is stopped and normal meeting\n// lifecycle rules take over.\nvar meetingReservationTTL = 5 * time.Minute\n\n// meetingInviteTTL 邀请有效期：超时未响应按过期处理（accept 迟到亦会被服务端拒绝）。\n// var 以便测试注入更短的 TTL。\nvar meetingInviteTTL = 60 * time.Second\n\n// meetingInvite 单次会议邀请状态机（inviteId → 状态），挂在 activeMeetingInvites 上。\ntype meetingInvite struct {\n\tInviteID   string\n\tMeetingID  string\n\tFrom       string // 房主 UserID（uniqId）\n\tTo         string // 被邀请方 UserID（uniqId）\n\tSourceRoom string // 原始 LetShare 房间号（与会议号分开，仅作定向投递通道）\n"}],"isError":false,"timestamp":1791315931368},"timestamp":"2026-10-06T19:45:31.372Z","cwd":"D:\\MyFile\\sample\\synchronous-github\\LetShare"}

{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"17c7aaae-2575-46c","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_00_1JdcvS4K1xcwXE4Anfww9242","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"\t_, ok := h.activeMeetingRooms.Load(roomID)\n\treturn ok\n}\n\nfunc (h *WebSocketHandler) isMeetingHost(roomID string, client *model.Client, meta *meetingMeta) bool {\n\tif meta == nil || client == nil || meta.Host != client.UniqID {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tif owner, joined := h.meetings.clientForUniqID(roomID, client.UniqID); joined {\n\t\treturn owner == client.ID\n\t}\n\treturn meta.HostClientID == client.ID\n}\n\n// cleanupMeetingState 客户端断线后的会议侧清理：\n//  1. 从所有 SFU 房间移除其参与者；房间变空则启动宽限定时（重入即取消），不立即回收——\n//     正常流程\"创建→路由跳转(WS 弹跳)→join\"依赖这一点。\n//  2. 房主登记了会议但从未进入（无 SFU 房间）：同样宽限后回收，防\"创建即泄漏\"。\nfunc (h *WebSocketHandler) cleanupMeetingState(client *model.Client) {\n\tif client == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\th.cleanupMeetingClientID(client.ID)\n}\n\n// cleanupMeetingClientID is also wired into WebSocketService's maintenance\n// cleanup path. This keeps meeting state correct when a connection is\n// removed by the generic inactivity sweeper rather than HandleWebSocket's\n// read loop.\nfunc (h *WebSocketHandler) cleanupMeetingClientID(clientID string) {\n\tremoved := h.meetings.leaveClient(clientID)\n\tfor _, member := range removed {\n\t\troomID := member.RoomID\n\t\troom, ok := h.sfuManager.GetRoom(roomID)\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\th.finishMeetingMemberLeave(roomID, member, clientID, room, false)\n\t}\n\t// 登记但从未进入的会议（房主断线）：宽限后回收。\n\th.activeMeetingRooms.Range(func(key, value any) bool {\n\t\troomID, _ := key.(string)\n\t\tmeta, ok := value.(*meetingMeta)\n\t\tif !ok || meta.HostClientID != clientID || meta.Parent != \"\" {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, roomExists := h.sfuManager.GetRoom(roomID); !roomExists {\n\t\t\th.scheduleMeetingEnd(roomID)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn true\n\t})\n}\n\n// scheduleMeetingEnd 为会议安排宽限期后自动结束：到点时若房间已有成员（重入）则跳过，\n// 否则通知+拆除+释放会议号。幂等：已有定时器时不重复安排。\nfunc (h *WebSocketHandler) scheduleMeetingEnd(roomID string) {\n\tv, ok := h.activeMeetingRooms.Load(roomID)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tmeta, _ := v.(*meetingMeta)\n\tif meta == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tmeta.mu.Lock()\n\tif meta.endTimer == nil {\n\t\tmeta.endTimer = time.AfterFunc(hostLeaveGrace, func() {\n\t\t\tif room, ok := h.sfuManager.GetRoom(roomID); ok && room.Count() > 0 {\n\t\t\t\treturn // 有人重入，会议继续\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\th.endMeetingRoom(roomID, \"timeout\")\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\tmeta.mu.Unlock()\n}\n\nfunc (h *WebSocketHandler) handleMeetingSDP(client *model.Client, message *model.WebSocketMessage) {\n\tif message.Channel == \"\" {\n\t\th.sendError(client, 400, \"meeting:sdp 缺少房间\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif !h.meetings.owns(message.Channel, client.UniqID, client.ID) {\n\t\th.sendChannelError(client, message.Channel, 403, \"meeting:sdp 不是当前会议连接\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tvar m meetingSDPMsg\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(message.Data, &m); err != nil {\n\t\th.sendChannelError(client, message.Channel, 400, \"meeting:sdp 数据格式错误: \"+err.Error())\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\troom, ok := h.sfuManager.GetRoom(message.Channel)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\th.sendChannelError(client, message.Channel, 400, \"meeting:sdp 尚未加入该会议房间\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tpart, ok := room.GetParticipant(client.UniqID)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\th.sendChannelError(client, message.Channel, 400, \"meeting:sdp 请先 meeting:join\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tswitch {\n\tcase m.Type == \"offer\" && m.To == \"\":\n\t\t// 客户端发布 offer（本人主 PC）\n\t\toffer := webrtc.SessionDescription{Type: webrtc.SDPTypeOffer, SDP: m.SDP}\n\t\tanswer, err := part.Offer(offer)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\th.sendChannelError(client, message.Channel, 500, \"meeting:sdp 生成 answer 失败: \"+err.Error())\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\treply := map[string]interface{}{\"type\": \"answer\", \"sdp\": answer.SDP, \"to\": client.UniqID}\n\t\th.sendMeetingToUser(message.Channel, client.UniqID, model.MessageTypeMeetingSDP, reply)\n\t\t// Pion 可能在 Offer 返回后异步触发 OnTrack；为避免“轨已注册但\n\t\t// 迟发布回调恰好错过”的竞态，发布成功后做一个有界最终一致性补偿。\n\t\tgo h.reconcileMeetingSubscriptionsEventually(message.Channel, client.UniqID)\n\n\tcase m.Type == \"offer\" && m.To != \"\":\n\t\t// 订阅请求：订阅者要求订阅 m.To 发布者；服务器建 Subscriber 并把其 offer 回发订阅者\n\t\tvar sub *sfu.Subscriber\n\t\tvar created bool\n\t\tvar err error\n\t\tif m.RestartID != \"\" {\n\t\t\tsub, created, err = part.RestartSubscription(m.To, m.RestartID)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tsub, created, err = part.SubscribeTo(m.To)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\th.sendChannelError(client, message.Channel, 400, \"meeting:sdp 订阅失败: \"+err.Error())\n\t\t\t// 发布者可能正在加入或刚完成 publish；不要让一次早到的请求\n\t\t\t// 决定最终结果，后续由 SFU 侧 reconcile 在 track 就绪后补推 offer。\n\t\t\tgo h.reconcileMeetingSubscriptionsEventually(message.Channel, m.To)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !created {\n\t\t\t// 幂等重试：若首个 offer 可能在网络中丢失，只重发仍在等待 answer\n\t\t\t// 的同一个 offer；稳定连接不重复协商。\n\t\t\tif offer, pending := sub.OfferForRetry(); pending {\n\t\t\t\th.sendMeetingSubscriberOfferWithRestartID(message.Channel, client.UniqID, m.To, offer, m.RestartID)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\t// 接入该订阅 PC 的本地候选，定向回发给订阅者（默认引擎非离线需真实 ICE）\n\t\tsub.OnICECandidate(func(c *webrtc.ICECandidate) {\n\t\t\th.forwardMeetingICE(message.Channel, client.UniqID, c, m.To)\n\t\t})\n\t\th.sendMeetingSubscriberOfferWithRestartID(message.Channel, client.UniqID, m.To, sub.Offer(), m.RestartID)\n\n\tcase m.Type == \"answer\" && m.To != \"\":\n\t\t// 订阅者回 answer（订阅 PC）：按发布者 m.To 找到对应 Subscriber\n\t\tsub, ok := part.GetSubscriber(m.To)\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\th.sendChannelError(client, message.Channel, 400, fmt.Sprintf(\"meeting:sdp 未找到订阅 %s 的连接\", m.To))\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := sub.SetRemoteDescription(webrtc.SessionDescription{Type: webrtc.SDPTypeAnswer, SDP: m.SDP}); err != nil {\n\t\t\th.sendChannelError(client, message.Channel, 500, \"meeting:sdp answer 设置失败: \"+err.Error())\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\t// 新轨道可能在上一 offer 等待 answer 期间到达。answer 确认后，\n\t\t// 生成并发送一个合并后的后续 offer，闭合音频/摄像头/屏幕的迟发布窗口。\n\t\tif next, ok, err := sub.PendingOffer(); err != nil {\n\t\t\th.sendChannelError(client, message.Channel, 500, \"meeting:sdp 后续 offer 生成失败: \"+err.Error())\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t} else if ok {\n\t\t\th.sendMeetingSubscriberOffer(message.Channel, client.UniqID, m.To, next)\n\t\t}\n\n\tcase m.Type == \"answer\" && m.To == \"\":\n\t\t// 发布 PC 的重协商 answer（少见）\n\t\tif err := part.SetRemoteDescription(webrtc.SessionDescription{Type: webrtc.SDPTypeAnswer, SDP: m.SDP}); err != nil {\n\t\t\th.sendChannelError(client, message.Channel, 500, \"meeting:sdp answer 设置失败: \"+err.Error())\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\n\tdefault:\n\t\th.sendChannelError(client, message.Channel, 400, \"meeting:sdp 未知的 type/direction 组合\")\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (h *WebSocketHandler) requireMeetingMember(client *model.Client, roomID, kind string) bool {\n"}],"isError":false,"timestamp":1791315932691},"timestamp":"2026-10-06T19:45:32.696Z","cwd":"D:\\MyFile\\sample\\synchronous-github\\LetShare"}

Keep the\n\t\t// host recipient bound to the current meeting connection; otherwise\n\t\t// host-only planes (minutes batches, summaries and control messages)\n\t\t// continue targeting the stale pre-reconnect client.\n\t\tif meta.Host == uniqID {\n\t\t\tmeta.HostClientID = client.ID\n\t\t}\n\t\tif meta.SourceRoom == \"\" && meta.Host == client.UniqID && sourceRoom != \"\" && h.wsService.RoomHasUniqID(sourceRoom, client.UniqID) {\n\t\t\tmeta.SourceRoom = sourceRoom\n\t\t}\n\t\tmeta.joined = true\n\t\tmeta.mu.Unlock()\n\t}\n\troom := h.sfuManager.JoinRoom(message.Channel)\n\t// 发布者中途新增 track（屏幕共享等）→ 通知所有已订阅者重协商\n\troom.SetOnTrackPublished(h.onMeetingTrackPublished)\n\t// 幂等：已存在则不重复建 PC\n\tif owner, joined := h.meetings.clientForUniqID(message.Channel, uniqID); joined && owner != client.ID {\n\t\th.releaseMeetingPresentation(message.Channel, uniqID)\n\t\t_ = room.RemoveParticipant(uniqID)\n\t}\n\t// 已关闭的参与者（PC failed/closed）必须显式摘除后才能重建：它的 fanout 已停摆，\n\t// 若复用，重连方会拿到一个「已关闭」的 participant，发布 offer 被拒、订阅绑到死轨，\n\t// 永远无法恢复（表现为接通方一直听不到对端、重连后仍静音直到挂断）。\n\tif stale, ok := room.GetParticipant(uniqID); ok && stale.IsClosed() {\n\t\t_ = room.RemoveParticipant(uniqID)\n\t}\n\tif existing, ok := room.GetParticipant(uniqID); ok && !existing.IsClosed() {\n\t\t_, previous := h.meetings.join(message.Channel, uniqID, userName, client.ID)\n\t\th.sendMeetingMembershipSnapshot(client.ID, message.Channel)\n\t\tif len(previous) > 0 {\n\t\t\th.broadcastMeetingMembership(message.Channel, \"leave\", uniqID, client.ID)\n\t\t\th.broadcastMeetingMembership(message.Channel, \"join\", uniqID, client.ID)\n\t\t}\n\t\th.sendMeetingInfo(message.Channel, uniqID, meta)\n\t\th.sendMeetingChatHistory(client, message.Channel)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tp, err := room.AddParticipant(uniqID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\th.sendError(client, 500, \"meeting:join 失败: \"+err.Error())\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\t// 接入该参与者发布 PC 的本地候选，定向回发给客户端\n\t_, previous := h.meetings.join(message.Channel, uniqID, userName, client.ID)\n\th.sendMeetingMembershipSnapshot(client.ID, message.Channel)\n\tif len(previous) > 0 {\n\t\th.broadcastMeetingMembership(message.Channel, \"leave\", uniqID, client.ID)\n\t}\n\th.broadcastMeetingMembership(message.Channel, \"join\", uniqID, client.ID)\n\tp.OnICECandidate(func(c *webrtc.ICECandidate) {\n\tfor _, p := range room.Participants() {\n\t\tif p.ID == publisherID {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif sub, ok := p.GetSubscriber(publisherID); Once a subscriber exists, only a pending offer is\n// resent; no duplicate peer connection is created.\nfunc (h *WebSocketHandler) reconcileMeetingSubscriptionsEventually(roomID, publisherID string) {\n\tfor attempt := 0; attempt < 10; attempt++ {\n\t\tif attempt > 0 {\n\t\t\ttime.Sleep(100 * time.Millisecond)\n\t\t}\n\t\th.reconcileMeetingSubscriptions(roomID, publisherID)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (h *WebSocketHandler) reconcileMeetingSubscriptions(roomID, publisherID string) {\n\troom, ok := h.sfuManager.GetRoom(roomID)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tpublisher, ok := room.GetParticipant(publisherID)\n\tif !ok || publisher.IsClosed() {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif len(publisher.PublishedTracks()) == 0 {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tfor _, subscriber := range room.Participants() {\n\t\tif subscriber.ID == publisherID || subscriber.IsClosed() {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tsub, created, err := subscriber.SubscribeTo(publisherID)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif created {\n\t\t\tsub.OnICECandidate(func(c *webrtc.ICECandidate) {\n\t\t\t\th.forwardMeetingICE(roomID, subscriber.ID, c, to/channel。\nfunc (h *WebSocketHandler) sendMeetingSubscriberOffer(roomID, subscriberID, publisherID string, offer webrtc.SessionDescription) {\n\th.sendMeetingSubscriberOfferWithRestartID(roomID, NonProSizeLimit 非 PRO 用户的文件大小上限\nconst NonProSizeLimit = 50 * 1024 * 1024 // 50MB\n\n// CreateTransferSession 创建文件传输会话\nfunc (fts *FileTransferService) CreateTransferSession(request *model.FileTransferRequest, isPro bool) (*model.FileTransferSession, error) {\n\t// 非 PRO 用户限制 50MB\n\tif !isPro && request.FileSize > NonProSizeLimit {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"文件大小 %.2f MB 超过 %.0f MB 限制，请升级到 PRO\",\n\t\t\tfloat64(request.FileSize)/(1024*1024), float64(NonProSizeLimit)/(1024*1024))\n\t}\n\n\t// PRO 统一上限 3GB\n\tif request.FileSize > fts.maxFileSize {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"文件大小超过限制: %d MB\", fts.maxFileSize/(1024*1024))\n\t}\n\n\t// 验证发送者和接收者\n\tif request.FromUserID == \"\" || request.ToUserID == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"必须指定发送者和接收者\")\n\t}\n\n\t// 设置默认分块大小\n\tif request.ChunkSize == 0 {\n\t\trequest.ChunkSize = fts.chunkSize\n\t}\n\n\t// 计算总块数\n\ttotalChunks := int(request.FileSize / int64(request.ChunkSize))\n\tif request.FileSize%int64(request.ChunkSize) != 0 {\n\t\ttotalChunks++\n\t}\n\tif totalChunks == 0 {\n\t\ttotalChunks = 1\n\t}\n\trequest.TotalChunks = totalChunks\n\n\t// 创建会话\n\tsession := &model.FileTransferSession{\n\t\tTransferID:   request.TransferID,\n\t\tFromUserID:   request.FromUserID,\n\t\tToUserID:     request.ToUserID,\n\t\tRoomName:     request.RoomName,\n\t\tFileName:     request.FileName,\n\t\tFileSize:     request.FileSize,\n\t\tFileType:     request.FileType,\n\t\tChunkSize:    request.ChunkSize,\n\t\tTotalChunks:  totalChunks,\n\t\tStartTime:    time.Now(),\n\t\tLastActivity: time.Now(),\n\t\tStatus:       \"pending\",\n\t}\n\n\tfts.sessionsMutex.Lock()\n\tfts.sessions[request.TransferID] = session\n\tfts.sessionsMutex.Unlock()\n\n\tlogrus.WithFields(logrus.Fields{\n\t\t\"transfer_id\": request.TransferID,\n\t\t\"file_name\":   request.FileName,\n\t\t\"file_size\":   request.FileSize,\n\t\t\"from_user\":   request.FromUserID,\n\t\t\"to_user\":     request.ToUserID,\n\t\t\"chunks\":      totalChunks,\n\t}).Info(\"创建文件传输会话\")\n\n\treturn session, nil\n}\n\n// GetSession 获取传输会话\n// 返回会话的快照拷贝（指针指向副本）。sessionsMutex 只保护 sessions 这个 map，\n// 不保护单个 session 的字段；若直接返回 map 内部指针，调用方在锁外读取字段会与\n// UpdateSessionStatus 等在锁内写字段产生数据竞争。快照拷贝让锁外读变为安全。\nfunc (fts *FileTransferService) GetSession(transferID string) (*model.FileTransferSession, error) {\n\tfts.sessionsMutex.RLock()\n\tdefer fts.sessionsMutex.RUnlock()\n\n\tsession, exists := fts.sessions[transferID]\n\tif !exists {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"传输会话不存在: %s\", transferID)\n\t}\n\n\tsnapshot := *session\n\treturn &snapshot, nil\n}\n\n// UpdateSessionStatus 更新会话状态\n// expectedStatus 为空时跳过 CAS 检查（best-effort），否则仅在当前状态匹配时才更新\nfunc (fts *FileTransferService) UpdateSessionStatus(transferID, expectedStatus, newStatus string) error {\n\tfts.sessionsMutex.Lock()\n\tdefer fts.sessionsMutex.Unlock()\n\n\tsession, exists := fts.sessions[transferID]\n\tif !exists {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"传输会话不存在: %s\", transferID)\n\t}\n\n\tif expectedStatus != \"\" && session.Status != expectedStatus {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"传输会话状态已变更: 期望 %s, 实际 %s\", expectedStatus, session.Status)\n\t}\n\n\tsession.Status = newStatus\n\tsession.LastActivity = time.Now()\n\n\tlogrus.WithFields(logrus.Fields{\n\t\t\"transfer_id\": transferID,\n\t\t\"status\":      newStatus,\n\t}).Debug(\"更新传输会话状态\")\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// UpdateSessionClients 更新会话的客户端ID\nfunc (fts *FileTransferService) UpdateSessionClients(transferID, fromClientID, toClientID string) error {\n\tfts.sessionsMutex.Lock()\n\tdefer fts.sessionsMutex.Unlock()\n\n\tsession, exists := fts.sessions[transferID]\n\tif !exists {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"传输会话不存在: %s\", transferID)\n\t}\n\n\tsession.FromClientID = fromClientID\n\tsession.ToClientID = toClientID\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// HandleClientDisconnect 处理客户端断开连接，清理该客户端作为发送方或接收方的活跃传输会话\nfunc (fts *FileTransferService) HandleClientDisconnect(clientID string) {\n\t// 先收集受影响的会话信息，避免锁内发送消息\n\ttype sessionInfo struct // 回退流带浏览器降噪，等价于 browser 模式正常路径\n195:      // 二次采集也失败：按今日回退行为上报，沿用首次合并流（无浏览器降噪但可用）\n202:// 确保状态类型正确\n241:  // 父组件\n247:  // 修改状态的类型，增加 \"video\"；默认不选任何发送模式（点击用户卡片 = 打开聊天）\n259:  // 右键“发送文件”后，文件选择器完成选择即发送给这个用户。\n261:  // 拖拽即传：悬停的用户卡片 + 待发区 chip 拖拽的原始文件\n265:  // ── 通话（语音/视频）状态与 CallManager 集成 — 纯增量，不影响现有功能 ──\n285:  // 来电铃声：incomingCall 出现播放、消失（接听/拒绝/超时）停止；卸载时统一停掉所有提示音\n308:  // ── 通话：CallManager 初始化（一次） ─────────────────────────────\n316:        // 3.7.0：信令通道不可用时拒绝拨号（避免创建必然失败的通话挂在界面）\n320:        // 视频能力偏好（编码器优先/码率上限）：从设置实时读取，每次建会话生效\n345:          // 通话结束（所有结束路径统一收口：主动挂断/对端挂断/超时/异常）：\n346:          // 停端侧降噪管线 —— 会话层无法停止管线持有的原始流轨与 AudioContext，\n347:          // 必须在此释放，否则麦克风持续被占用；stop() 幂等，无管线时无副作用\n349:          // outgoing 播放拨号回铃，incoming 播放来电铃；进入 connecting/reconnecting 后两端提示铃都停止。\n351:          // 函数式更新：同一批次内多个事件（如 onRemoteStream 后紧跟 state=active）\n352:          // 依赖 activeCallRef.current 会互相覆盖，必须链式基于最新 state 合并\n358:            // 防御：浏览器可能把 audio/video 拆成两个 stream 且 video 先到，\n359:            // 此时后到的音频流若被丢弃即无声 —— 把缺失 kind 的轨道并入现有流\n360:            // （保持 stream 引用稳定，UI 绑定不重挂）。\n367:                // 触发重渲染（同一流对象，UI effect 依赖引用不变，需要手动 kick）\n370:              return prev; // 轨道已齐（重复事件），不动\n382:          // 对端挂断/通话中断：自动关闭面板 + Discord 式\"嘟\"提示音（自动关闭已由置 null 承担）\n384:          // 3.7.0 结束原因文案：去电超时/对方未接听/忙线 给出明确提示（对标 Discord）\n399:    // 对端离开（页面关闭/刷新广播 leave）：立即结束与其的通话（其 bye 已不可能到达）\n401:    // 后台省流定时器豁免：通话/视频进行中注入活跃查询，后台 10 分钟到点也不断开连接\n423:    // 3.8.x：拨号前探测对端 WS 在线（统一在线状态，通话与文件传输共用）。\n424:    // 离线用户不发 invite、不占麦克风，直接提示。\n431:      // 统一采集层：语音/视频全部经 acquireCallStreams（3A 显式约束 + 首选麦克风 +\n432:      // contentHint + nsMode 统一分流，视频合并采集不再绕过端侧降噪管线）\n436:      // 视频通话合并采集失败已由采集层内部降级：按返回流视频轨数量判定 videoEnabled\n457:      // 同步 seed ref：ontrack/状态回调可能在 React effect 同步 ref 前触发（同 acceptIncoming 竞态）\n471:      // 统一采集层：语音/视频全部经 acquireCallStreams（3A 显式约束 + 首选麦克风 +\n472:      // contentHint + nsMode 统一分流，视频合并采集不再绕过端侧降噪管线）\n476:      // 视频来电合并采集失败已由采集层内部降级：按返回流视频轨数量判定 videoEnabled\n478:      // 竞态修复：ontrack 会在 acceptCall 内部（SRD 处理缓冲 offer）同步触发，\n479:      // onRemoteStream 回调此刻就要能查到 activeCall —— 必须先提交状态并同步 seed ref，\n480:      // 否则远端流被丢弃，被叫端远端 track 无 sink → 浏览器不启动 NetEq 渲染 → 单通。\n519:    playDisconnectTone(); // 本端挂断同款提示音（与对端挂断一致）\n540:  // 连接质量采样（CallBar 质量徽标）：经 ref 取当前通话 peer 委托 CallManager.getQuality。\n541:  // useCallback 固定身份，避免 ActiveCallPanel 轮询 interval 随渲染反复重建。\n548:  // 中继状态采样（CallBar 传输徽标）：SFU 通话才返回 true/false（实际 ICE 选中是否为 relay），\n549:  // P2P 通话返回 null（transport 已明示直连）。null = 未确定，UI 展示中性“直连”标签。\n563:  // 中继状态轮询：每 3s 采样一次并写回 activeCall.isRelayed；面板关闭即停\n578:  // 通话中换麦克风：重新采集首选麦克风 → replaceTrack 原子换轨（不动协商），旧轨停止。\n582:    if (!cur) return; // 未在通话：仅保存偏好，下次通话生效\n587:      // 换麦重采同样走统一采集层按降噪模式分流（实验室模式走端侧管线重建），避免换轨后设置被静默重置\n591:      if (cur.muted) newTrack.enabled = false; // 保持静音状态\n594:        newTrack.stop(); // 无活跃会话/替换失败：停止新采集的轨，不泄漏麦克风\n597:      // 停掉旧音频轨并更新 state（保留旧流视频轨）。\n598:      // 会话侧 swapAudioTrack 已清旧轨 onended，这里 stop() 不会误触发 hangup(\"error\")。\n608:  // 通话中切换降噪模式：先按新模式采好替换流，再按需停端侧管线并原子换轨。\n609:  // 实验室模式建图/重建期间旧发送轨约 1s 无声（wasm 加载 + addModule），通话中切换可接受。\n612:    if (!cur) return; {"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"17c7aaae-2575-46c","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_00_99wYgykkG4QS1LPyNq7Z1018","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"    callManagerRef.current = manager;\n    meetingManager.setBusyProvider(() => manager.isInCall());\n    realTimeColab.registerCallSignalHandler((from, data) => manager.handleSignal(from, data));\n    realTimeColab.registerCallSFUHandler((type, data, channel) => manager.handleSfuSignal(type, data, channel));\n    // 对端离开（页面关闭/刷新广播 leave）：立即结束与其的通话（其 bye 已不可能到达）\n    realTimeColab.registerCallPeerLeaveHandler((peerId) => manager.peerLeft(peerId));\n    // 后台省流定时器豁免：通话/视频进行中注入活跃查询，后台 10 分钟到点也不断开连接\n    realTimeColab.registerCallActivityProvider(() => manager.isInCall());\n    return () => {\n      realTimeColab.registerCallActivityProvider(null);\n      realTimeColab.registerCallSFUHandler(null);\n      meetingManager.setBusyProvider(null);\n      manager.leaveRoom();\n      callManagerRef.current = null;\n    };\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, []);\n\n  const connectedUsersRef = React.useRef<ConnectedUser[]>([]);\n  React.useEffect(() => { connectedUsersRef.current = connectedUsers; }, [connectedUsers]);\n\n  const startCall = React.useCallback(async (peerId: string, media: CallMedia) => {\n    const manager = callManagerRef.current;\n    if (!manager) return;\n    if (manager.isInCall(peerId)) {\n      alertUseMUI(t(\"call.alreadyInCall\", \"该用户已在通话中\"), 2000, { kind: \"info\" });\n      return;\n    }\n    // 3.8.x：拨号前探测对端 WS 在线（统一在线状态，通话与文件传输共用）。\n    // 离线用户不发 invite、不占麦克风，直接提示。\n    const online = await realTimeColab.isPeerOnline(peerId, 2500).catch(() => false);\n    if (!online) {\n      alertUseMUI(t(\"call.peerOffline\", \"对方已离线，暂无法呼叫\"), 2200, { kind: \"info\" });\n      return;\n    }\n    try {\n  const collectDirFiles = async (entry: FileSystemEntry, relativeDir: string, out: DirEntryFile[]): Promise<void> => {\n    if (entry.isFile) {\n      const file = await new Promise<File | null>((resolve) => {\n        (entry as FileSystemFileEntry).file(resolve, () => resolve(null));\n      });\n      if (file) out.push({ file, relPath: relativeDir ? `${relativeDir}/${file.name}` : file.name });\n      return;\n    }\n    if (!entry.isDirectory) return;\n    const reader = (entry as FileSystemDirectoryEntry).createReader();\n    const nextRelativeDir = relativeDir ? `${relativeDir}/${entry.name}` : entry.name;\n    for (;;) {\n      const batch = await new Promise<FileSystemEntry[]>((resolve) => {\n        reader.readEntries(resolve, () => resolve([]));\n      });\n      if (batch.length === 0) break; // 必须循环读取，直到空批次才结束\n      for (const child of batch) {\n        await collectDirFiles(child, nextRelativeDir, out);\n      }\n    }\n  };\n\n  /**\n   * 将 DataTransfer 解析为可传输的文件列表：\n   * - 拖入文件夹 → 打包为 LetShare_<ts>_<文件夹名>.zip（保留目录结构）\n   * - 0 字节的空文件 / 空文件夹 → 拒绝并提示（不允许空文件传输）\n   * 返回 null 表示没有可传输内容（已提示）。\n   */\n  const dropDataToTransferables = async (dt: DataTransfer): Promise<File[] | null> => {\n    const entries: FileSystemEntry[] = [];\n    const items = dt.items;\n    if (items) {\n      for (const item of Array.from(items)) {\n        const entry = (item as unknown as { webkitGetAsEntry?: () => FileSystemEntry | null }).webkitGetAsEntry?.();\n        if (entry) entries.push(entry);\n      }\n    }\n    if (entries.length === 0) {\n      // 不支持目录遍历的浏览器（如 Firefox）：退回 dataTransfer.files，过滤空文件\n      const raw = Array.from(dt.files ?? []).filter(f => f.size > 0);\n      if (raw.length === 0) {\n        alertUseMUI(t('toast.emptyFileNotAllowed'), 2500, { kind: \"warning\" });\n        return null;\n      }\n      return raw;\n    }\n\n    const transferables: File[] = [];\n    let rejected = 0;\n    for (const entry of entries) {\n      if (entry.isDirectory) {\n        const collected: DirEntryFile[] = [];\n        await collectDirFiles(entry, \"\", collected);\n        if (collected.length === 0) {\n          rejected++;\n          continue; // 空文件夹不允许传输\n        }\n        const { default: JSZip } = await import(\"jszip\");\n        const zip = new JSZip();\n        collected.forEach(({ file, relPath }) => zip.file(relPath, file));\n        const content = await zip.generateAsync({ type: \"blob\" });\n"}],"isError":false,"timestamp":1791315955050},"timestamp":"2026-10-06T19:45:55.054Z","cwd":"D:\\MyFile\\sample\\synchronous-github\\LetShare"}

Keep the board session alive, but stop following it so\n    // the active bar can offer “继续跟随共享人”. A server-forced board focus\n    // remains non-dismissable by design.\n    if (whiteboardOn && followPresenter) {\n      setFollowPresenter(false);\n      whiteboardHiddenRef.current = true;\n      setLegacyWhiteboardOn(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const audioRef = useRef<HTMLAudioElement | null>(null);\n  const remoteAudioTrackKey = state.remoteTracks\n    .filter((remote) => remote.kind === \"audio\" && remote.track)\n    .map((remote) => remote.track!.id)\n    .join(\"|\");\n  const remoteAudioStream = useMemo(() => {\n    const stream = new MediaStream();\n    for (const remote of state.remoteTracks) {\n      if (remote.kind === \"audio\" && remote.track) stream.addTrack(remote.track);\n    }\n    return stream;\n  }, [state.remoteTracks]);\n  const remoteAudioPipelineRef = useRef<RemoteAudioPipeline | null>(null);\n  const previousRemoteAudioTrackKeyRef = useRef<string | null>(null);\n  const previousMediaSettingsVersionRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const audioTracksChanged = previousRemoteAudioTrackKeyRef.current !== remoteAudioTrackKey;\n    const settingsChanged = previousMediaSettingsVersionRef.current !== mediaSettingsVersion;\n    previousRemoteAudioTrackKeyRef.current = remoteAudioTrackKey;\n    previousMediaSettingsVersionRef.current = mediaSettingsVersion;\n    // Video/screen tracks are stored in the same remoteTracks array. They must\n    // not tear down an otherwise healthy remote audio decoder/playback graph.\n    if (!audioTracksChanged && !settingsChanged) return;\n\n    const audio = audioRef.current;\n    const pipeline = (remoteAudioPipelineRef.current ??= new RemoteAudioPipeline());\n    pipeline.detach();\n    if (remoteAudioStream.getAudioTracks().length === 0) {\n      if (audio) audio.srcObject = null;\n      return;\n    }\n    const active = pipeline.attach(remoteAudioStream, {\n      volume: Number(settingsStore.get(\"speakerVolume\") ?? 1),\n      clarity: settingsStore.get(\"voiceClarityEnabled\") ?? true,\n      widen: settingsStore.get(\"spatialAudioEnabled\") ?? false,\n    });\n    const sinkId = settingsStore.get(\"speakerDeviceId\") ?? \"\";\n    if (active) {\n      pipeline.resume();\n      if (sinkId) void pipeline.setSinkId(sinkId);\n      if (audio) {\n        // Keep the same hidden native sink used by ordinary calls. /**\n  * 发送文件给用户（P2P方式）\n  */\n  public async sendFileToUser(\n   id: string,\n   file: File\n   // onProgress?: (progress: number) => void\n  ): Promise<void> {\n  const channel = channel is not available.\");\n  }\n\n  const peer = RealTimeColab.peers.get(id);\n  const chunkSize = getEffectiveDataChannelChunkSize({\n   desiredChunkSize: this.transferConfig.chunkSize,\n transferConfig.chunkSize));\n  const maxConcurrentReads = transferConfig.maxConcurrentReads;\n  const transferId = `p2p_${Date.now()}_${this.generateUUID()}`;\n  let chunksSent = 0;\n  let bytesSent = this.p2pSendingTransferIds.set(id, transferId);\n  this.startActiveOutgoingFileTransfer({\n   transferId,\n   fileName: file.name,\n   fileSize: file.size,\n   targetUserId: id,\n   transport: \"p2p\",\n  });\n  this.setDownloadPageState(true);\n  this.setFileTransferProgress(0);\n  this.setFileTransferStatus(t('alert.p2pSendingFile'), \"info\", { showPanel: false });\n\n  const stillOwnsTransfer = () => this.p2pSendingTransferIds.get(id) === transferId;\n  const isCurrentTransfer = () =>\n   isP2PSendTransferCurrent({\n    expectedTransferId: transferId,\n    currentTransferId: this.p2pSendingTransferIds.get(id),\n    globallyAborted: this.aborted,\n   });\n\n  // ── ACK 看门狗：接收端周期回传分块 ACK；本地 send 成功但对端毫无 ACK 进度\n  //    即为僵死通道（对应「卡在 totalChunks,\n   chunkSize: transferConfig.chunkSize,\n   // 3.8.2：hash 在全部块发送完成后以 file-hash 控制帧补发（见发送循环尾部）\n   hashPending: true,\n  };\n  console.debug(\"[P2P FILE] transfer config\", {\n peer?.sctp?.maxMessageSize,\n  });\n\n  const readChunk = (index: number): Promise<ArrayBuffer> => {\n   const readOperation = new Promise<ArrayBuffer>((resolve, reject) => {\n    if (!isCurrentTransfer()) return reject(new Error(\"Reading aborted\"));\n\n    const offset = index * transferConfig.chunkSize;\n    const slice = file.slice(\n     offset,\n     offset + transferConfig.chunkSize\n    );\n    slice.arrayBuffer().then((result) => {\n     if (!isCurrentTransfer()) return reject(new Error(\"Reading aborted\"));\n     resolve(result);\n    }, reject);\n   });\n\n   return withTransferTimeout(readOperation, {\n    timeoutMs: 15_000,\n    timeoutMessage: t('alert.readTimeout'),\n   });\n  };\n\n  const sendChunk = async (\n   index: number,\n   options: { countProgress?: boolean } = {}\n  ) => {\n   const countProgress = options.countProgress ?? true;\n   if (!isCurrentTransfer()) return;\n\n   const chunkBuffer = await readChunk(index);\n   if (!isCurrentTransfer()) return;\n\n   // 只在首传时喂 hash（重传块已在首传时计入，避免重复累积）\n   if (countProgress) {\n    hasher.update(new Uint8Array(chunkBuffer));\n   }\n\n   const bufferWithHeader = index,\n"}],"isError":false,"timestamp":1791315957864},"timestamp":"2026-10-06T19:45:57.868Z","cwd":"D:\\MyFile\\sample\\synchronous-github\\LetShare"}

{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"17c7aaae-2575-46c","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_01_PZjMACnMtbRtIQhWxlRf0996","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"   return this.hasActiveCall();\n  }\n\n  // ─── WS 自动重连（3.7.0：断线自愈，对标 Discord）──────────────────\n  private reconnectTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;\n  private reconnectAttempt = 0;\n  /** 意外断开才自动重连；主动 disconnect()（后台省流等）置 false 后永不静默重连 */\n  private autoReconnectAllowed = false;\n\n  /** 指数退避调度（1/2/4/8/16/30s 封顶 + 校验房间）——只服务意外断开场景。 */\n  private scheduleReconnect(): void {\n   if (!this.autoReconnectAllowed || this.reconnectTimer != null) return;\n   const delay = reconnectDelayMs(this.reconnectAttempt);\n   this.reconnectAttempt += 1;\n   settingsStore.updateUnrmb(\"serverConnState\", \"reconnecting\");\n   console.debug(`[ColabLib] 自动重连 ${delay}ms 后（第 ${this.reconnectAttempt} 次）`);\n   this.reconnectTimer = setTimeout(() => {\n    this.reconnectTimer = null;\n    void this.attemptReconnect();\n   }, delay);\n  }\n\n  private cancelReconnect(): void {\n   if (this.reconnectTimer == null) return;\n   clearTimeout(this.reconnectTimer);\n   this.reconnectTimer = null;\n  }\n\n private async attemptReconnect(): Promise<void> {\n   if (!this.autoReconnectAllowed) return;\n   // A direct Meeting route deliberately has no ordinary LetShare roomId.\n   // Reconnect that authenticated transport independently; otherwise one\n   // transient websocket close permanently kills the meeting session.\n   const transportOnly = this.transportOnlyConnection;\n   const roomId = transportOnly ? \"\" : settingsStore.get(\"roomId\");\n   if (!transportOnly && (!roomId || !validateRoomName(roomId).isValid)) return; // 无有效房间不空转\n   // silent：失败不弹 toast、不打扰，返回值经 connectToServer 内部再次 schedule（退避递增）\n   await this.connectToServer({ silent: true, transportOnly });\n }\n\n  // ─── 在线探活 / 拨号前探测（3.8.x，通话与文件传输统一消费同一份状态）──────────\n  /**\n   * 周期性探活：对\"非 disconnected、且未建立活跃 P2P data channel\"的用户\n   * 每 5s 发一次服务器层 ping；每个 ping 经过完整超时后才计失败，连续 3 次无 pong → 判定离线，\n   * 走 handleUserLeave 从 userList 移除（UI 随 updateUI 消失，通话/传输同时失效）。\n   * 有活跃 P2P 通道的用户用通道心跳（现有机制）即可，不重复探测。\n   */\n  private startPresenceProbe(): void {\n   const PROBE_INTERVAL = 5_000;\n   const PONG_TIMEOUT = 15_000;\n   const MAX_FAILS = 3;\n   const probe = (): void => {\n    for (const [id, user] of this.userList.entries()) {\n     if (user.status === \"disconnected\") continue;\n     // 已有活跃 P2P data channel 的用户：走通道心跳（现有 lastPing/lastPong），不重复 WS 探活\n     const channel = this.dataChannels.get(id);\n     const peer = RealTimeColab.peers.get(id);\n     if (channel?.readyState === \"open\" && peer?.connectionState === \"connected\") {\n      this.userProbeFails.set(id, 0);\n      this.userServerProbeTs.delete(id);\n      continue;\n     }\n     const now = Date.now();\n     const decision = decidePresenceProbe(now, {\n      lastPongAt: this.userServerPongTs.get(id) ?? 0,\n      lastProbeAt: this.userServerProbeTs.get(id) ?? 0,\n      failures: this.userProbeFails.get(id) ?? 0,\n     }, PONG_TIMEOUT, MAX_FAILS);\n     if (!decision.shouldProbe) continue;\n\n     this.broadcastSignal({ type: \"ping\", to: id, ts: now });\n     this.userServerProbeTs.set(id, now);\n     this.userProbeFails.set(id, decision.failures);\n     if (decision.shouldRemove) {\n      this.userProbeFails.delete(id);\n      this.userServerPongTs.delete(id);\n      this.userServerProbeTs.delete(id);\n      console.warn(`[PRESENCE] ${id} 探活连续 ${decision.failures} 次无回应，判定离线`);\n      this.handleUserLeave({ from: id });\n     }\n    }\n   };\n   setInterval(probe, PROBE_INTERVAL);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * 拨号前探测：对端 WS 是否在线（发 ping 等 pong，带 2.5s 超时）。\n   * - userList 已无此人 → 直接离线；\n   * - 10s 内刚收到过 pong → 免往返直接在线；\n   * - 否则发 ping 轮询 userServerPongTs，超时判离线。\n   * 通话与文件传输统一用此判定（同一 userServerPongTs）。\n"}],"isError":false,"timestamp":1791315966590},"timestamp":"2026-10-06T19:46:06.596Z","cwd":"D:\\MyFile\\sample\\synchronous-github\\LetShare"}

err != nil {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 400, \"数据格式错误\", \"\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 防御性检查：data为空对象时可能是客户端重连导致的异常消息，跳过处理\n\tif len(data) == 0 {\n\t\tlogrus.WithField(\"client_id\", client.ID).Debug(\"ACCEPT消息data字段为空，跳过处理\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\ttransferID, ok := data[\"transfer_id\"].(string)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 400, \"缺少transfer_id\", \"\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tsession, err := h.fileTransferService.GetSession(transferID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 404, \"传输会话不存在\", transferID)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 验证接收者\n\tif !clientOwnsTransferUser(client, session.ToUserID) {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 403, \"无权接受此传输\", transferID)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 验证状态转换合法性\n\tif session.Status != \"pending\" {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 400, \"传输会话状态错误，无法接受: \"+session.Status, transferID)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 更新会话状态\n\th.fileTransferService.UpdateSessionStatus(transferID, \"pending\", \"accepted\")\n\th.fileTransferService.UpdateSessionClients(transferID, session.FromClientID, client.ID)\n\n\t// 通知发送者\n\tacceptData := map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\"transfer_id\": transferID,\n\t}\n\tif flowControl, ok := data[\"flow_control\"].(string); ok && flowControl != \"\" {\n\t\tacceptData[\"flow_control\"] = flowControl\n\t}\n\tif ackEveryChunks, ok := data[\"ack_every_chunks\"].(float64); ok && ackEveryChunks > 0 {\n\t\tacceptData[\"ack_every_chunks\"] = int(ackEveryChunks)\n\t}\n\tif ackWindowChunks, ok := data[\"ack_window_chunks\"].(float64); ok && ackWindowChunks > 0 {\n\t\tacceptData[\"ack_window_chunks\"] = int(ackWindowChunks)\n\t}\n\n\tacceptMsg := model.NewWebSocketMessage(\n\t\tmodel.MessageTypeFileTransferAccept,\n\t\tsession.RoomName,\n\t\t\"\",\n\t\tacceptData,\n\t)\n\n\th.fileTransferService.SendMessageToUser(session.FromUserID, session.RoomName, acceptMsg)\n\n\tlogrus.WithField(\"transfer_id\", transferID).Info(\"文件传输已接受\")\n}\n\n// handleFileTransferReject 处理拒绝文件传输\nfunc (h *WebSocketHandler) handleFileTransferReject(client *model.Client, message *model.WebSocketMessage) {\n\tvar data map[string]interface{}\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(message.Data, &data); err != nil {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 400, \"数据格式错误\", \"\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 防御性检查：data为空对象时可能是客户端重连导致的异常消息，跳过处理\n\tif len(data) == 0 {\n\t\tlogrus.WithField(\"client_id\", client.ID).Debug(\"REJECT消息data字段为空，跳过处理\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\ttransferID, ok := data[\"transfer_id\"].(string)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 400, \"缺少transfer_id\", \"\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tsession, err := h.fileTransferService.GetSession(transferID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 404, \"传输会话不存在\", transferID)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif !clientOwnsTransferUser(client, session.ToUserID) {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 403, \"无权拒绝此传输\", transferID)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 验证状态转换合法性\n\tif session.Status != \"pending\" {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 400, \"传输会话状态错误，无法拒绝: \"+session.Status, transferID)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 更新会话状态\n\th.fileTransferService.UpdateSessionStatus(transferID, \"pending\", \"rejected\")\n\n\t// 通知发送者\n\trejectMsg := model.NewWebSocketMessage(\n\t\tmodel.MessageTypeFileTransferReject,\n\t\tsession.RoomName,\n\t\t\"\",\n\t\tmap[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\"transfer_id\": transferID,\n\t\t\t\"reason\":      data[\"reason\"],\n\t\t},\n"}],"isError":false,"timestamp":1791315973438},"timestamp":"2026-10-06T19:46:13.442Z","cwd":"D:\\MyFile\\sample\\synchronous-github\\LetShare"}

err != nil {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 400, \"数据格式错误\", \"\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 防御性检查：data为空对象时可能是客户端重连导致的异常消息，跳过处理\n\tif len(data) == 0 {\n\t\tlogrus.WithField(\"client_id\", client.ID).Debug(\"START消息data字段为空，跳过处理\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\ttransferID, ok := data[\"transfer_id\"].(string)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 400, \"缺少transfer_id\", \"\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tsession, err := h.fileTransferService.GetSession(transferID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 404, \"传输会话不存在\", transferID)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 验证发送者\n\tif !clientOwnsTransferUser(client, session.FromUserID) {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 403, \"无权开始此传输\", transferID)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 验证状态转换合法性\n\tif session.Status != \"accepted\" && session.Status != \"resending\" {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 400, \"传输会话状态错误，无法开始传输: \"+session.Status, transferID)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 更新会话状态\n\th.fileTransferService.UpdateSessionStatus(transferID, session.Status, \"transferring\")\n\n\t// 通知接收者\n\tstartMsg := model.NewWebSocketMessage(\n\t\tmodel.MessageTypeFileTransferStart,\n\t\tsession.RoomName,\n\t\t\"\",\n\t\tmap[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\"transfer_id\":  transferID,\n\t\t\t\"file_name\":    session.FileName,\n\t\t\t\"file_size\":    session.FileSize,\n\t\t\t\"total_chunks\": session.TotalChunks,\n\t\t},\n\t)\n\n\th.fileTransferService.SendMessageToUser(session.ToUserID, session.RoomName, startMsg)\n\n\tlogrus.WithField(\"transfer_id\", transferID).Info(\"文件传输已开始\")\n}\n\n// handleFileTransferEnd 处理文件传输完成\nfunc (h *WebSocketHandler) handleFileTransferEnd(client *model.Client, message *model.WebSocketMessage) {\n\tvar data map[string]interface{}\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(message.Data, &data); err != nil {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 400, \"数据格式错误\", \"\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 防御性检查：data为空对象时可能是客户端重连导致的异常消息，跳过处理\n\tif len(data) == 0 {\n\t\tlogrus.WithField(\"client_id\", client.ID).Debug(\"END消息data字段为空，跳过处理\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\ttransferID, ok := data[\"transfer_id\"].(string)\n\tif !ok || transferID == \"\" {\n\t\t// 记录data内容帮助排查移动端重连导致transfer_id丢失的问题\n\t\tlogrus.WithFields(logrus.Fields{\n\t\t\t\"client_id\": client.ID,\n\t\t\t\"data_keys\": getMapKeys(data),\n\t\t}).Warn(\"END消息缺少transfer_id，可能是客户端重连导致data字段不完整\")\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 400, \"缺少transfer_id\", \"\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tsession, err := h.fileTransferService.GetSession(transferID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 404, \"传输会话不存在\", transferID)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 验证发送者。END 只表示发送端已经发完字节，不能代表接收端已经组装成功。\n\tif !clientOwnsTransferUser(client, session.FromUserID) {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 403, \"无权结束此传输\", transferID)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 验证状态转换合法性\n\tif session.Status != \"completed\" && session.Status != \"transferring\" && session.Status != \"resending\" && session.Status != \"interrupted\" {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 400, \"传输会话状态错误，无法结束传输: \"+session.Status, transferID)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tif session.Status == \"completed\" {\n\t\tlogrus.WithField(\"transfer_id\", transferID).Debug(\"ignore late transfer end for completed transfer\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\th.fileTransferService.UpdateSessionStatus(transferID, session.Status, \"ending\")\n\n\t// 只通知接收者进入收尾校验；发送者必须等待接收方 COMPLETE。\n"}],"isError":false,"timestamp":1791315973438},"timestamp":"2026-10-06T19:46:13.443Z","cwd":"D:\\MyFile\\sample\\synchronous-github\\LetShare"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"17c7aaae-2575-46c","type":"assistant","message":{"role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Now I have a critical finding: the server's ACCEPT handler allows **only** `pending`. The client's relay flow: sender sends REQUEST → server creates session (status pending) → receiver REJECT/ACCEPT → accepted → sender sends START → transferring. Good.\n\nBut client `handleTransferAccept`: `if (session.status !== \"pending\") { ignore }`. And the server's accept guard `session.Status != \"pending\"` → \"传输会话状态错误，无法接受\". Now, the client resend flow: when the client-side receiver's 30s timeout triggers a resend, the server sets status to \"resending\" (if status is transferring/ending/interrupted). Then the client's `handleTransferEnd`... it's fine.\n\nMore important: The client `acceptTransfer` is called after user accepts. If the server session was cleared (e.g., 10-min stale cleanup or client reconnect where sessions were... no). Also client `handleTransferRequest`→`showAcceptDialog`→ auto-accept → send ACCEPT. If server already removed the session (e.g., the sender's REQUEST error path removed it), the ACCEPT gets 404 → file:transfer:error → client kills session. Fine.\n\nAnother copy-fork: client-side `handleTransferAccept` requires session.status === \"pending\" else ignore silently; server requires \"pending\" else send error. Different behavior (ignore vs error) — a copy fork in the same handshake. Minor.\n\nNow, another divergence: server ACCEPT forwards `flow_control`, `ack_every_chunks`, `ack_window_chunks` to the sender. Client's `handleTransferAccept` type is `{ transfer_id: string; flow_control?: string; ack_window_chunks?: number }` — reads flow_control and only uses ack_every/ack_window? It sets session.flowControl and ackedChunks = 0, but doesn't read ack_every_chunks/ack_window_chunks from the accept (uses its own constants). So the negotiated window is ignored by the sender. Actually the sender uses RELAY_ACK_WINDOW_CHUNKS = 24 constant, ignoring the receiver's advertised ack_window_chunks. That's a protocol divergence: receiver advertises ack_every_chunks=8/ack_window_chunks=24, sender hardcodes 24 and ignores the receiver's advertised values. Since both are 24/8, it works today, but the negotiation is dead. Good finding (medium).\n\nAlso the receiver's `maybeSendReceiverAck` uses RELAY_ACK_EVERY_CHUNKS=8 while P2P uses 16 → inconsistency in ACK pacing between transports (already noted).\n\nNow, websocket.go media frame: read 3760-3860 for handleMediaFrame, and the 24-byte header/64KB limit. Also check `mediaFrameMaxPayload` enforcement location. And check binary message size limits in the server read (wsSetReadLimit). {"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"17c7aaae-2575-46c","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_00_VHegC0xxNlYGTkEhLoPB4484","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 文件传输二进制：前256字节是JSON元数据头,剩余是数据\n\tif len(data) < 256 {\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 400, \"二进制消息格式错误\", \"\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 解析元数据（前256字节，去除padding的0）\n\tvar chunkMeta model.FileTransferChunk\n\tmetaBytes := bytes.TrimRight(data[:256], \"\\x00\")\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(metaBytes, &chunkMeta); err != nil {\n\t\tlogrus.WithField(\"client_id\", client.ID).WithError(err).Error(\"解析文件块元数据失败\")\n\t\th.sendFileTransferError(client, 400, \"文件块元数据格式错误\", \"\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 提取实际数据(跳过元数据部分)，但转发时保留完整帧，接收端可直接按 transfer_id 定位会话\n\tchunkData := data[256:]\n\th.handleFileChunk(client, &chunkMeta, chunkData, data)\n}\n\n// handleMediaFrame 处理通话媒体帧（公网兜底轨道）。\n// 帧头 layout（24B，与客户端 encodeMediaFrame 一致）：\n//\n//\t[0..4)   \"medi\" 魔数\n//\t[4..20)  callId (ascii, \\0 填充)\n//\t[20..22) seq (uint16 BE)\n//\t[22)     track: 0=audio 1=video 2=data\n//\t[23)     padding\n//\n// 转发语义：盲转发。服务器不解析 payload，不做会话管理、不做重传。\n// 目标用户定位：媒体帧是 1对1 通话，发送方所在房间 + 头内 callId 关联的对端。\n// 由于服务器不维护通话会话表，这里采用\"房间内除发送方外的全部用户\"广播，\n// 由客户端按 callId 过滤丢弃非本通话帧（与信令 publish 广播同语义，零会话状态）。\nfunc (h *WebSocketHandler) handleMediaFrame(client *model.Client, frame []byte) {\n\tif len(frame) < mediaFrameHeaderSize {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tpayloadLen := len(frame) - mediaFrameHeaderSize\n\tif payloadLen > mediaFrameMaxPayload {\n\t\tlogrus.WithFields(logrus.Fields{\n\t\t\t\"client_id\": client.ID,\n\t\t\t\"payload\":   payloadLen,\n\t\t}).Warn(\"媒体帧 payload 超过上限，丢弃\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// 发送方必须已加入某个房间才能定位对端\n\tvar roomName string\n\tfor r := range client.Rooms {\n\t\troomName = r\n\t\tbreak\n\t}\n\tif roomName == \"\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// callId 仅用于日志，不解析 payload\n\tcallID := mediaFrameCallID(frame)\n\tseq := mediaFrameSeq(frame)\n\ttrack := frame[18]\n\n\t// 转发给房间内除发送方外的全部用户（客户端按 callId 过滤）\n\tforwarded := 0\n\tfor _, target := range h.wsService.GetRoomClients(roomName) {\n\t\tif target.ID == client.ID {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := h.wsService.WriteBinaryToClient(target, frame); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlogrus.WithFields(logrus.Fields{\n\t\t\t\t\"operation\": \"media.forward\",\n\t\t\t\t\"call_id\":   callID,\n\t\t\t\t\"seq\":       seq,\n\t\t\t\t\"track\":     track,\n\t\t\t\t\"target\":    target.UserID,\n\t\t\t\t\"room\":      roomName,\n\t\t\t\t\"error\":     err.Error(),\n\t\t\t}).Debug(\"媒体帧转发失败\")\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tforwarded++\n\t}\n\n\tlogrus.WithFields(logrus.Fields{\n\t\t\"operation\": \"media.forward\",\n\t\t\"call_id\":   callID,\n\t\t\"seq\":       seq,\n\t\t\"track\":     track,\n\t\t\"payload\":   payloadLen,\n\t\t\"room\":      roomName,\n\t\t\"forwarded\": forwarded,\n\t}).Debug(\"媒体帧已转发\")\n}\n\n// mediaFrameCallID 从媒体帧头提取 callId（offset 4, ascii, 遇 \\0 截断）\nfunc mediaFrameCallID(frame []byte) string {\n\tif len(frame) < 4+mediaFrameCallIDBytes {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n=== READ LIMIT ===\n26:// wsWriteBufferPool 共享写缓冲池，50个连接共用而非各自占 64KB\n27:var wsWriteBufferPool = &sync.Pool{\n39:\tReadBufferSize: 65536,\n41:\tWriteBufferPool: wsWriteBufferPool,\n446:\tconn.SetReadLimit(8 * 1024 * 1024)                         // meeting snapshots may contain bounded Excalidraw image data\n"}],"isError":false,"timestamp":1791315978680},"timestamp":"2026-10-06T19:46:18.684Z","cwd":"D:\\MyFile\\sample\\synchronous-github\\LetShare"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"17c7aaae-2575-46c","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_01_6UwHicEMaTX9SZmOpXa55912","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"\t\t\t\tclient.Metadata[\"isPro\"] = true\n\t\t\t\tlogrus.WithFields(logrus.Fields{\n\t\t\t\t\t\"client_id\": clientID,\n\t\t\t\t\t\"user_id\":   userID,\n\t\t\t\t}).Debug(\"PRO token 验证成功\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else if err != nil {\n\t\t\tlogrus.WithFields(logrus.Fields{\n\t\t\t\t\"client_id\": clientID,\n\t\t\t\t\"error\":     err.Error(),\n\t\t\t}).Debug(\"PRO token 验证失败\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// 添加到服务\n\th.wsService.AddClient(client)\n\n\tlogrus.WithFields(logrus.Fields{\n\t\t\"client_id\": clientID,\n\t\t\"user_id\":   userID,\n\t}).Info(\"WebSocket客户端已连接\")\n\n\t// 使用defer确保资源清理，即使发生panic也能执行\n\tdefer func() {\n\t\t// 恢复panic，防止整个服务崩溃\n\t\tif r := recover(); r != nil {\n\t\t\tlogrus.WithFields(logrus.Fields{\n\t\t\t\t\"client_id\": clientID,\n\t\t\t\t\"panic\":     r,\n\t\t\t}).Error(\"WebSocket处理发生panic\")\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// 确保连接被关闭\n\t\tif conn != nil {\n\t\t\tconn.Close()\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// 从服务中移除客户端（这会清理所有相关资源）\n\t\th.wsService.RemoveClient(clientID)\n\t\t// 会议侧资源清理：SFU 参与者、空房会议号释放、房主宽限定时\n\t\th.cleanupMeetingState(client)\n\n\t\tlogrus.WithField(\"client_id\", clientID).Info(\"WebSocket连接已清理\")\n\t}()\n\n\t// 设置连接参数\n\tconn.SetReadLimit(8 * 1024 * 1024)                         // meeting snapshots may contain bounded Excalidraw image data\n\tconn.SetReadDeadline(time.Now().Add(websocketReadTimeout)) // Chrome 后台节流需宽容\n\tconn.SetPongHandler(func(string) error {\n\t\tconn.SetReadDeadline(time.Now().Add(websocketReadTimeout))\n\t\tclient.LastPing = time.Now()\n\t\treturn nil\n\t})\n\n\t// 启动ping定时器\n\tticker := time.NewTicker(30 * time.Second)\n\tdefer ticker.Stop()\n\n\t// 启动消息处理goroutine\n\tdone := make(chan struct{}, 1)\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tdefer func() {\n\t\t\tif r := recover(); r != nil {\n\t\t\t\tlogrus.WithFields(logrus.Fields{\n\t\t\t\t\t\"client_id\": clientID,\n\t\t\t\t\t\"panic\":     r,\n\t\t\t\t}).Error(\"消息处理goroutine发生panic\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Signal the owner loop without risking a second close/send.\n\t\t\tselect {\n\t\t\tcase done <- struct{}{}:\n\t\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}()\n\t\th.handleMessages(client, conn)\n\t}()\n\n\t// 保持连接和定期ping\n\tfor {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase <-done:\n\t\t\t// 消息处理goroutine结束，退出主循环\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\tcase <-ticker.C:\n\t\t\t// 检查连接是否仍然有效\n\t\t\tif conn == nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// 使用defer recover防止panic\n\t\t\tfunc() {\n\t\t\t\tdefer func() {\n\t\t\t\t\tif r := recover();
