{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"17c7aaae-2575-46c","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_00_ePAn9wBEPXghMO38uJ879501","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"12: *   [18)     track: 0=audio 1=video 2=data\n123:export const MEDIA_FRAME_HEADER_SIZE = 24;\n130://   [22)     track: 0=audio 1=video 2=data\n136:const TRACK_OFFSET = SEQ_OFFSET + 2; // 22\n149:  track: MediaTrackId;\n160:  const buf = new ArrayBuffer(MEDIA_FRAME_HEADER_SIZE + frame.payload.byteLength);\n174:  // [22) track\n175:  bytes[TRACK_OFFSET] = frame.track;\n177:  bytes.set(new Uint8Array(frame.payload), MEDIA_FRAME_HEADER_SIZE);\n182:  if (buf.byteLength < MEDIA_FRAME_HEADER_SIZE) {\n200:  const track = bytes[TRACK_OFFSET];\n201:  if (track !== MediaTrack.audio && track !== MediaTrack.video && track !== MediaTrack.data) {\n202:    throw new Error(`unknown media track ${track}`);\n204:  const payload = buf.slice(MEDIA_FRAME_HEADER_SIZE);\n205:  return { callId, seq, track, payload };\n"}],"isError":false,"timestamp":1791315983344},"timestamp":"2026-10-06T19:46:23.348Z","cwd":"D:\\MyFile\\sample\\synchronous-github\\LetShare"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"17c7aaae-2575-46c","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_01_27HcsMoOzlSmGiyf8yuq9293","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"// ─── 媒体帧编解码（公网兜底轨道）─────────────────────────────────────\n\nexport const MEDIA_FRAME_HEADER_SIZE = 24;\nexport const CALL_ID_BYTES = 16;\n\n// 帧头布局（24B，与服务器 websocket.go handleMediaFrame 一致）：\n//   [0..4)   \"medi\" 魔数（服务器据此区分媒体帧与文件传输帧）\n//   [4..20)  callId (ascii, \\0 填充)\n//   [20..22) seq (uint16 BE)\n//   [22)     track: 0=audio 1=video 2=data\n//   [23)     padding\nconst MEDIA_MAGIC = \"medi\";\nconst MEDIA_MAGIC_LEN = 4;\nconst CALL_ID_OFFSET = MEDIA_MAGIC_LEN;\nconst SEQ_OFFSET = CALL_ID_OFFSET + CALL_ID_BYTES; // 20\nconst TRACK_OFFSET = SEQ_OFFSET + 2; // 22\n\nexport const MediaTrack = {\n  audio: 0,\n  video: 1,\n  data: 2,\n} as const;\n\nexport type MediaTrackId = (typeof MediaTrack)[keyof typeof MediaTrack];\n\nexport type MediaFrame = {\n  callId: string;\n  seq: number;\n  track: MediaTrackId;\n  payload: ArrayBuffer;\n};\n\nexport function encodeMediaFrame(frame: MediaFrame): ArrayBuffer {\n  if (!isValidCallId(frame.callId) || frame.callId.length > CALL_ID_BYTES) {\n    throw new Error(\"callId too long for media frame header\");\n  }\n  if (!Number.isInteger(frame.seq) || frame.seq < 0 || frame.seq > 0xffff) {\n    throw new Error(\"seq must be uint16\");\n  }\n  const buf = new ArrayBuffer(MEDIA_FRAME_HEADER_SIZE + frame.payload.byteLength);\n  const view = new DataView(buf);\n  const bytes = new Uint8Array(buf);\n\n  // [0..4) 魔数\n  for (let i = 0; i < MEDIA_MAGIC_LEN; i++) {\n    bytes[i] = MEDIA_MAGIC.charCodeAt(i);\n  }\n  // [4..20) callId\n  for (let i = 0; i < frame.callId.length; i++) {\n    bytes[CALL_ID_OFFSET + i] = frame.callId.charCodeAt(i) & 0xff;\n  }\n  // [20..22) seq (BE)\n  view.setUint16(SEQ_OFFSET, frame.seq, false);\n  // [22) track\n  bytes[TRACK_OFFSET] = frame.track;\n  // [23) padding 已是 0\n  bytes.set(new Uint8Array(frame.payload), MEDIA_FRAME_HEADER_SIZE);\n  return buf;\n}\n\nexport function decodeMediaFrame(buf: ArrayBuffer): MediaFrame {\n  if (buf.byteLength < MEDIA_FRAME_HEADER_SIZE) {\n    throw new Error(\"media frame too short\");\n  }\n  const bytes = new Uint8Array(buf);\n  const view = new DataView(buf);\n  // 魔数校验（防御性：非媒体帧不应走到这里）\n  for (let i = 0; i < MEDIA_MAGIC_LEN; i++) {\n    if (bytes[i] !== MEDIA_MAGIC.charCodeAt(i)) {\n      throw new Error(\"not a media frame (bad magic)\");\n    }\n  }\n  let callId = \"\";\n  for (let i = 0; i < CALL_ID_BYTES; i++) {\n    const b = bytes[CALL_ID_OFFSET + i];\n    if (b === 0) break;\n    callId += String.fromCharCode(b);\n  }\n  const seq = view.getUint16(SEQ_OFFSET, false);\n  const track = bytes[TRACK_OFFSET];\n"}],"isError":false,"timestamp":1791315983344},"timestamp":"2026-10-06T19:46:23.348Z","cwd":"D:\\MyFile\\sample\\synchronous-github\\LetShare"}

{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"17c7aaae-2575-46c","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_00_nvYeeCfsWrogj2hbfA8T0104","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"     1\t/**\n     2\t * WebSocket 自动重连退避策略（纯函数，Node 单测可导）。\n     3\t *\n     4\t * colabLib 无法在 Node 中直接 import（依赖浏览器 API/巨型单例），\n     5\t * 指数退避与其他纯逻辑单独成文件，便于 tests/ 直接断言。\n     6\t */\n     7\t\n     8\texport const RECONNECT_BASE_MS = 1_000;\n     9\texport const RECONNECT_CAP_MS = 30_000;\n    10\t\n    11\t/**\n    12\t * 第 attempt 次（0 起）重连的延迟：1s → 2s → 4s → 8s → 16s → 30s 封顶。\n    13\t * 抖动由调用方叠加（避免多客户端齐步重连）。\n    14\t */\n    15\texport function reconnectDelayMs(attempt: number): number {\n    16\t  const a = Math.max(0, Math.floor(attempt));\n    17\t  return Math.min(RECONNECT_BASE_MS * 2 ** a, RECONNECT_CAP_MS);\n    18\t}=== presenceProbe ===\n     1\texport interface PresenceProbeState {\n     2\t  lastPongAt: number;\n     3\t  lastProbeAt: number;\n     4\t  failures: number;\n     5\t}\n     6\t\n     7\texport interface PresenceProbeDecision {\n     8\t  shouldProbe: boolean;\n     9\t  failures: number;\n    10\t  shouldRemove: boolean;\n    11\t}\n    12\t\n    13\t/**\n    14\t * Decide whether a server ping should be sent.\n    15\t * A probe must be given a full timeout window before it counts as failed;\n    16\t * otherwise the first probe is incorrectly counted as a failure immediately.\n    17\t */\n    18\texport function decidePresenceProbe(\n    19\t  now: number,\n    20\t  state: PresenceProbeState,\n    21\t  timeoutMs = 15_000,\n    22\t  maxFailures = 3,\n    23\t): PresenceProbeDecision {\n    24\t  if (state.lastPongAt > 0 && now - state.lastPongAt <= timeoutMs) {\n    25\t    return { shouldProbe: true, failures: 0, shouldRemove: false };\n    26\t  }\n    27\t\n    28\t  if (state.lastProbeAt > 0 && now - state.lastProbeAt < timeoutMs) {\n    29\t    return { shouldProbe: false, failures: state.failures, shouldRemove: false };\n    30\t  }\n    31\t\n    32\t  const failures = state.failures + 1;\n    33\t  return {\n    34\t    shouldProbe: true,\n    35\t    failures,\n    36\t    shouldRemove: failures >= maxFailures,\n    37\t  };\n    38\t}\n"}],"isError":false,"timestamp":1791316001279},"timestamp":"2026-10-06T19:46:41.284Z","cwd":"D:\\MyFile\\sample\\synchronous-github\\LetShare"}
{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"17c7aaae-2575-46c","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_01_46Zv3hVwfTvnItyW0TLs3526","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"// NewErrorRateLimiter 创建错误频率限制器\nfunc NewErrorRateLimiter() *ErrorRateLimiter {\n\treturn &ErrorRateLimiter{\n\t\tclients: make(map[string]*clientErrorRecord),\n\t}\n}\n\n// Allow 检查是否允许向指定客户端发送错误消息\n// 5秒内超过3条错误则屏蔽10秒，避免重连风暴\nfunc (r *ErrorRateLimiter) Allow(clientID string) bool {\n\tr.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer r.mu.Unlock()\n\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\trecord, exists := r.clients[clientID]\n\tif !exists {\n\t\tr.clients[clientID] = &clientErrorRecord{\n\t\t\ttimestamps: []time.Time{now},\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\n\t// 检查是否处于屏蔽期\n\tif !record.blockedUntil.IsZero() && now.Before(record.blockedUntil) {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\t// 清理5秒前的过期时间戳\n\tcutoff := now.Add(-5 * time.Second)\n\tvalid := record.timestamps[:0]\n\tfor _, t := range record.timestamps {\n\t\tif t.After(cutoff) {\n\t\t\tvalid = append(valid, t)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\trecord.timestamps = append(valid, now)\n\n\t// 5秒内超过3条错误，屏蔽10秒\n\tif len(record.timestamps) > 3 {\n\t\trecord.blockedUntil = now.Add(10 * time.Second)\n\t\trecord.timestamps = nil // 重置计数\n\tminutesFlushTimer *time.Timer\n\tchatHistory       []meetingChatEntry\n}\n\ntype meetingChatEntry struct {\n\tFrom string `json:\"from\"`\n\tText string `json:\"text\"`\n\tTs   int64  `json:\"ts\"`\n\tTo   string `json:\"to,omitempty\"`\n}\n\ntype meetingPresentationState struct {\n\t// Mode/OwnerID remain the derived active-stage fields consumed by older\n\t// clients. They are derived from the independent screen/whiteboard state\n\t// below and must never be used as the storage for both capabilities.\n\tMode                 string `json:\"mode\"`\n\tBoardMode            string `json:\"boardMode\"`\n\tOwnerID              string `json:\"ownerId\"`\n\tEpoch                uint64 `json:\"epoch\"`\n\tScreenOwnerID        string `json:\"screenOwnerId\"`\n\tScreenEpoch          uint64 `json:\"screenEpoch\"`\n\tWhiteboardActive     bool   `json:\"whiteboardActive\"`\n\tWhiteboardLeaderID   string `json:\"whiteboardLeaderId\"`\n\tWhiteboardEpoch      uint64 `json:\"whiteboardEpoch\"`\n\tWhiteboardVisible    bool   `json:\"whiteboardVisible\"`\n\tWhiteboardForceOpen  bool   `json:\"whiteboardForceOpen\"`\n\tWhiteboardFocusEpoch uint64 `json:\"whiteboardFocusEpoch\"`\n\tFocusTarget          string `json:\"focusTarget\"`\n\tFocusEpoch           uint64 `json:\"focusEpoch\"`\n\t// PresenterID is the single shared-person lease. Screen and whiteboard\n\t// publishers remain independent sources, while followers use the\n\t// presenter's target to decide which source to show.\n\tPresenterID             string                     `json:\"presenterId\"`\n\tPresenterEpoch          uint64                     `json:\"presenterEpoch\"`\n\tPresenterTarget         string                     `json:\"presenterTarget\"` // screen / whiteboard / camera\n\tPresenterFollowEpoch    uint64                     `json:\"presenterFollowEpoch\"`\n\tWhiteboardViewport      *meetingWhiteboardViewport `json:\"whiteboardViewport,omitempty\"`\n\tWhiteboardViewportEpoch uint64                     `json:\"whiteboardViewportEpoch\"`\n}\n\ntype meetingWhiteboardViewport struct {\n\tCenterX float64 `json:\"centerX\"`\n\tCenterY float64 `json:\"centerY\"`\n\tZoom    float64 `json:\"zoom\"`\n\tEpoch   uint64  `json:\"epoch\"`\n}\n\ntype meetingExcalidrawState struct {\n\tRevision       uint64\n\tScene          json.RawMessage\n\tOperations     map[string]uint64\n\tOperationOrder []string\n}\n\n// meetingMinutesState intentionally contains no provider API key. Keys stay in\n// the host browser and are never placed on the meeting WebSocket.\ntype meetingMinutesState struct {\n\tConfigured      bool            `json:\"configured\"`\n\tRunning         bool            `json:\"running\"`\n\tRequireConsent  bool            `json:\"requireConsent\"`\n\tAsrSource       string          `json:\"asrSource,omitempty\"`\n\tAsrModel        string          `json:\"asrModel,omitempty\"`\n\tSummaryProvider string          `json:\"summaryProvider,omitempty\"`\n\tSummaryModel    string          `json:\"summaryModel,omitempty\"`\n\tSummary         string          `json:\"summary,omitempty\"`\n\tConsented       map[string]bool `json:\"-\"`\n"}],"isError":false,"timestamp":1791316001280},"timestamp":"2026-10-06T19:46:41.284Z","cwd":"D:\\MyFile\\sample\\synchronous-github\\LetShare"}

{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"17c7aaae-2575-46c","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_00_LYUTov0MbQlzpRmf1Ri94754","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"\tlogrus.WithField(\"transfer_id\", transferID).Info(\"文件传输已取消\")\n}\n\n// notifyTransferError 通知传输错误\nfunc (h *WebSocketHandler) notifyTransferError(session *model.FileTransferSession, errMsg string) {\n\th.fileTransferService.UpdateSessionStatus(session.TransferID, \"\", \"error\")\n\n\terrorMsg := model.NewWebSocketMessage(\n\t\tmodel.MessageTypeFileTransferError,\n\t\tsession.RoomName,\n\t\t\"\",\n\t\tmap[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\"transfer_id\": session.TransferID,\n\t\t\t\"error\":       errMsg,\n\t\t},\n\t)\n\n\tif err := h.fileTransferService.SendMessageToUser(session.FromUserID, session.RoomName, errorMsg); err != nil {\n\t\tlogrus.WithFields(logrus.Fields{\n\t\t\t\"transfer_id\": session.TransferID,\n\t\t\t\"user_id\":     session.FromUserID,\n\t\t\t\"error\":       err.Error(),\n\t\t}).Warn(\"通知发送方传输错误失败\")\n\t}\n\tif err := h.fileTransferService.SendMessageToUser(session.ToUserID, session.RoomName, errorMsg); err != nil {\n\t\tlogrus.WithFields(logrus.Fields{\n\t\t\t\"transfer_id\": session.TransferID,\n\t\t\t\"user_id\":     session.ToUserID,\n\t\t\t\"error\":       err.Error(),\n\t\t}).Warn(\"通知接收方传输错误失败\")\n\t}\n\n\t// 延迟清理会话，给客户端时间处理错误消息\n\tgo func() {\n\t\ttime.Sleep(5 * time.Second)\n\t\th.fileTransferService.RemoveSession(session.TransferID)\n\t}()\n}\n\n// getMapKeys 返回map的所有key，用于调试日志（如排查transfer_id丢失问题）\nfunc getMapKeys(data map[string]interface{}) []string {\n\tkeys := make([]string, 0, len(data))\n\tfor k := range data {\n\t\tkeys = append(keys, k)\n\t}\n\treturn keys\n}\n\nfunc parseRequestedChunkIndexes(value interface{}, missingCountValue interface{}, totalChunks int) ([]int, error) {\n\tindexes, provided, err := parseChunkIndexes(value)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tif !provided {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\tif totalChunks <= 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid total_chunks for resend\")\n\t}\n\tif len(indexes) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"chunk_indexes cannot be empty\")\n"}],"isError":false,"timestamp":1791316008265},"timestamp":"2026-10-06T19:46:48.269Z","cwd":"D:\\MyFile\\sample\\synchronous-github\\LetShare"}

{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"17c7aaae-2575-46c","type":"assistant","message":{"role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Confirmed: `notifyTransferError` doesn't check the limiter and sends directly to both users. But `sendFileTransferError` (used in the same branch before it) does — so double error messages to the same client on forward failure: one throttled (`file:transfer:error`) then notifyTransferError → `SendMessageToUser` (not throttled) → sends the same error type again → duplicate error toasts + \"transfer session already error\" on client. Actually client's handleTransferError deletes sessions; a second error with the same transfer_id: completedTransferIds doesn't include it; sessions already deleted; so sendingSessions.get → undefined; it still calls `alertUseMUI(...transferError...)` unconditionally → duplicate toast. Minor duplicate-notification quirk.\n\nAlso: `notifyTransferError` sets status to \"error\" and removes the session 5s later, but the client's error path is `sendFileTransferError(400, ...)` for chunk-while-terminal → the client treats it as fatal (handleTransferError deletes local sessions and toasts). Confirms my earlier concern: any server-side status guard mismatch → client's local transfer is aborted. Combined with the `resending` guard gap on RESEND, this yields \"文件传不完\".\n\nI have a lot. Now let me fill a few remaining slots:\n1. share.tsx call race region 457-500 (already grepped, good text) — need file:line exact: 457-480.\n2. colabLib 2170-2200 (hasher from session creation), 2900-2910, 2560-2570 done, 3158 done, 3990-4000, 2335.\n3. colabLib 925-930 (channel routing P0 root cause) — line numbers confirmed.\n4. websocket.go: chat text cap 1954, 2016 member checks, 3438, 2060? And `unknownBinaryTransferIssueKeys` cleared only on connect-lost/new request. Fine.\n\nOne more high-value area: ServerFileTransfer `finalizeReceivedFile` sets `completedTransferIds.add` but never removes → grows unbounded over a long session; plus `handleTransferEnd` early return for completed ids. Low severity.\n\nAnother: `handleConnectionLost` does not clear `completedTransferIds` but sets `lastDisconnectTime`; `cancelAllSendingSessions` also doesn't clear it. Minor.\n\nAlso `ServerFileTransfer.handleConnectionLost` clears receiving sessions and aborts directSink (which closes the file picker writable → partially written file left on disk without indication). Actually abort() → partially written file left. Client shows error. Fine.\n\nNow check the `handleBinaryData` \"chunk without transfer → CANCEL\" behavior: when a receiver client reconnect happens and the server still relays (session was created before), the late chunk arrives with no local session → client sends CANCEL to the server, which would tear down the *sender's* relay session! That would kill a transfer that the server still considers valid. Since the resend/resume path exists (interrupted → resume-state), the CANCEL proactively kills. Hmm: the client sends CANCEL with `channel` undefined? `sendTransferControlMessage(type, transferId, reason)` with channel undefined → server handles cancel via transfer_id lookup regardless of channel. {"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"17c7aaae-2575-46c","type":"assistant","message":{"role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Big finding: the client never calls `requestResumeState` (defined but unused!). So the client has no way to query resume state despite the server sending `file:transfer:resume-state` on peer disconnect and the client having a RESUME_STATE handler. Let me verify with a repo-wide grep for `requestResumeState` — the earlier grep over `src/` only found the definition at 322. So it's dead code (never invoked). Combined: server sends resume-state proactively after a peer disconnect, and the client's `handleTransferResumeState` handles it, but the receiver's client can never actively resume because:\n- client `handleTransferResumeState` only sets `sendingSession.ackedChunks` (sender path) and shows \"interrupted\" toast; there's no path to re-send missing chunks nor to re-accept.\nLet me double check handleTransferResumeState: it only touches sendingSession and displays status. So on interruption, the relay session is marked interrupted server-side and the receiver must... nothing. The client cannot recover a relay transfer after a WS drop: `handleConnectionLost` clears all local sessions and `completedTransferIds` remain; on reconnect the receiver has no session, so late chunks → \"chunk without transfer\" → CANCEL. That's a coherent story: **relay transfers cannot survive a reconnect at all** despite the server having a full spool + resume-state machinery. That's THE high-value finding for the rewrite (\"文件传不完\" after any network blip).\n\nEvidence:\n- ServerFileTransfer.ts:958-996 handleConnectionLost clears all sessions unconditionally.\n- ServerFileTransfer.ts:322 requestResumeState never called (dead).\n- ServerFileTransfer.ts:1826 handleTransferResumeState only updates the sender's ack counter; no resume action.\n- websocket.go:3943/4643 notifyTransferError.\n- Server HandleClientDisconnect marks interrupted + retains spool.\n\nAlso client `cancelAllSendingSessions` called on visibility-visible reconnect.\n\nLet me verify requestResumeState is truly unused across the repo (including tests) and check whether share.tsx/MeetingRoom wire a resume UI. grep whole repo.","thinkingSignature":"reasoning_content"},{"type":"toolCall","id":"call_00_UmOzK6111F9M36IUOZ979813","name":"bash","arguments":{"command":"cd \"D:/MyFile/sample/synchronous-github/LetShare\" && grep -rn \"requestResumeState\" src/ tests/ server/ 2>/dev/null | head; echo \"--- test dir ---\"; ls tests/ 2>/dev/null | head -40","timeout":30}},{"type":"toolCall","id":"call_01_IjGibeU1Nf7XXKB8fTbE0916","name":"bash","arguments":{"command":"cd \"D:/MyFile/sample/synchronous-github/LetShare\" && grep -rn \"requestResumeState\\|resume\" tests/*.ts tests/**/*.ts 2>/dev/null | head -20; echo \"--- ls tests ---\"; ls -R tests 2>/dev/null | head -60","timeout":30}}],"api":"openai-completions","provider":"deepseek","model":"deepseek-flash","usage":{"input":570,"output":684,"cacheRead":138752,"cacheWrite":0,"reasoning":461,"totalTokens":140006,"cost":{"input":0.00017099999999999998,"output":0.0008208,"cacheRead":0.000832512,"cacheWrite":0,"total":0.0018243119999999998}},"stopReason":"toolUse","timestamp":1791316021904,"responseId":"9cb9b362-d3e4-457b-88be-23b4ee23375f","rawStopReason":"tool_calls","thinkingLevel":"high"},"timestamp":"2026-10-06T19:47:07.221Z","cwd":"D:\\MyFile\\sample\\synchronous-github\\LetShare"}

{"isSidechain":true,"agentId":"17c7aaae-2575-46c","type":"toolResult","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_01_Q5W7wpC4s8ShPYX8JpwW2522","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"\t}, client.UniqID)\n}\n\n// handleMeetingChat 会议内实时聊天：服务器纯转发（不落盘、不存历史）。\n//   - 无 to：房间广播（与会成员可见）。\n//   - 带 to：定向私聊 —— 服务器校验发送者和目标都是当前会议成员\n//     （以 SFU 房间参与者为准，meeting:join 后才算成员），\n//     禁止跨会议、跨房间投递（目标只按 meetingID 房间内查找）。\n//\n// 文本长度服务端封顶，防止小水管被大包滥用。\nfunc (h *WebSocketHandler) appendMeetingChatHistory(roomID string, entry meetingChatEntry) {\n\tv, ok := h.activeMeetingRooms.Load(roomID)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tmeta, ok := v.(*meetingMeta)\n\t}\n\troom, ok := h.sfuManager.GetRoom(roomID)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\th.sendError(client, 400, \"meeting:chat 尚未加入该会议房间\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\t// 发送者必须是当前会议成员（SFU 参与者；防跨会议、跨房间伪造投递）\n\tmemberIDs := h.meetingParticipantIDs(roomID)\n\tif !memberIDs[client.UniqID] {\n\t\th.sendError(client, 403, \"meeting:chat 发送者不是该会议成员\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tvar m struct {\n\t\tText string `json:\"text\"`\n\t\tTo   string `json:\"to\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(message.Data, &m); err != nil {\n\t\th.sendError(client, 400, \"meeting:chat 数据格式错误\")\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\ttext := strings.TrimSpace(m.Text)\n\tif text == \"\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif len(text) > meetingChatMaxLen {\n\t\ttext = text[:meetingChatMaxLen]\n\t}\n\n\t// 定向私聊：目标必须仍是本会议成员（roomID == meetingID，杜绝跨会议/跨房间）\n\tif m.To != \"\" {\n\t\tif m.To == client.UniqID {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !memberIDs[m.To] {\n\t\t\th.sendError(client, 404, \"目标成员不在当前会议中\")\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tentry := meetingChatEntry{From: client.UniqID, Text: text, Ts: time.Now().UnixMilli(), To: m.To}\n\t\tdelivered := h.sendMeetingToUser(roomID, m.To, model.MessageTypeMeetingChat, entry)\n\t\tif !delivered {\n\t\t\th.sendError(client, 404, \"目标成员已离开会议\")\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\th.appendMeetingChatHistory(roomID, entry)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tentry := meetingChatEntry{From: client.UniqID, Text: text, Ts: time.Now().UnixMilli()}\n\th.appendMeetingChatHistory(roomID, entry)\n\th.broadcastMeetingMembers(roomID, room, model.MessageTypeMeetingChat, entry, client.UniqID) // 排除发送者：其 UI 已本地回显，省一次回环\n}\n\t\th.sendError(client, 400, \"meeting:invite 目标用户无效\")\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tsourceRoom := m.SourceRoom\n\tif sourceRoom == \"\" {\n\t\th.sendError(client, 400, \"meeting:invite 缺少原始房间号\")\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\t// 发送者必须当前仍在原始房间（原始房间只提供在线名单与投递通道）。\n\tif !h.wsService.RoomHasUniqID(sourceRoom, client.UniqID) {\n\t\th.sendError(client, 403, \"未连接到原始房间，无法发送邀请\")\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\t// 同一（会议, 目标）已存在未过期且待处理的邀请时拒绝重复发送。\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\th.inviteMu.Lock()\n"}],"isError":false,"timestamp":1791316037557},"timestamp":"2026-10-06T19:47:17.562Z","cwd":"D:\\MyFile\\sample\\synchronous-github\\LetShare"}

`websocket.go:1951-1957`；实现 `2004-2060`、`1972-2010`、`246`(`meetingChatHistoryMax = 200`) | 按注释重写会让新加入成员看不到聊天历史（受 `sendMeetingChatHistory` 支撑的功能被删） | 中 |\n| 聊天文本按 **2000 字节**截断（`text[:2000]`） | | 锁外读与会话写产生数据竞争（只有 `-race` 能抓到）；症状是偶发读到撕裂状态（状态跳到终态、进度回退） | 中 |\n| `ForwardChunkToReceiver` 写接收方必须在**释放会话锁之后**（后续 sendProgressUpdate→sendToClient 会再次加锁） | `file_transfer.go:620-628` | 持锁写会自我死锁（Go 的 sync.Mutex 不可重入）→ 转发 goroutine 全挂（传输永久卡住但连接还在） | 高 |\n| 找不到接收者时 100ms × 3 次重试，并在多个同 user 连接中选 `LastPing` 最新的 | `file_transfer.go:780-800` | 移动端重连后旧 socket / 30s、5s | `file_transfer.go:823`、`websocket.go:4396`、`file_transfer.go:4675` 附近 | 部分（30s/5s 无注释） |\n\n---\n\n## 给重写的三条最高风险提示（按投入产出排序）\n\n1. **relay 的\"可恢复\"是假的**：服务端有 spool + `interrupted` + `resume-state`，客户端却（a）`handleConnectionLost` 无条件清空会话、（b）`requestResumeState` 死代码、（c）收到未知分片主动 CANCEL、（d）`resume-state` 不做任何动作。四处叠加 ⇒ 任何一次网络抖动都必然整包重传。这是\"文件传不完\"的单一最大来源，且是 `ServerFileTransfer.ts` + `tests/serverRelayResumeProtocol.test.ts`（假绿）共同掩盖的。\n2. **状态机白名单不一致（F1+F2）可以直接把一次正常重传判成致命错误**：RESEND 不接受 `resending`，而客户端把 `file:transfer:error` 一律当终止处理。重写必须把四张白名单收敛成一张表 + 显式合法转移图，并用行为测试（而非 grep 源码）锁住。\n3. **媒体帧的两个隐患只在特定拓扑/时序出现**：`websocket.go:3822` 的 `frame[18]`（文档/客户端是 22）与 `websocket.go:3811` 的\"随机挑一个房间\"。后者会在\"原房间 + 会议房间并存\"的用户上产生间歇性单通，属于最难复现的一类，务必在重写时改为按 callId → 会话 → 目标 channel
